This work presents ARTA, a lightweight reinforcement-learning-based throttling mechanism that detects and suppresses RowHammer activity by monitoring fine-grained memory access behavior within the DRAM refresh window (t_REFW) and dynamically adjusting core throughput using a Q-learning frequency scaling governor. ARTA requires no DRAM-side hardware modification or offline training, using small SRAM structures in the memory controller -- a per-core, per-bank FIFO queue (CBF) and a compact Q-table -- for immediate deployment. Our evaluation shows that ARTA eliminates all bitflips at N_BO values down to 64, reduces bitflips up to 22K times at N_BO of 20, and improves performance up to 73.6% over state-of-the-art mitigation mechanisms by limiting preventive action overheads for improved memory bandwidth throughput. These results demonstrate that adaptive RL-based throttling provides robust, scalable, and high-performance RowHammer mitigation for emerging DRAM systems.

</details>


### [144] [SPARX: Secure and Privacy-Aware Approximate CNN Acceleration with Edge RISC-V SoC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09946)
*Sonu Kumar,Akash Sankhe,Mukul Lokhande,Santosh Kumar Vishvakarma*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 论文提出了SPARX框架，通过定制的RISC-V指令扩展、近似对数加速器以及安全隐私机制，实现了高性能、低功耗且具备隐私保护意识的边缘CNN推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘AI设备在严格的能量、性能、安全和隐私约束下，需要实时的CNN推理，而现有的近似计算加速器缺乏对安全与隐私的综合考量。

Method: 1. 架构：开发了基于RV32IMC RISC-V SoC的SPARX框架；2. 计算：采用近似对数计算单元，并通过迭代对数乘法器（ILM）进行优化；3. 安全：集成轻量级差分噪声隐私引擎与质询-响应认证机制；4. 决策：提出了一套包含ASI、AE、QoA、AFOM和HAE的近似分析与决策框架。

Result: 基于ILM的加速器相比精确Radix-4 Booth乘法器，面积减少51.7%，功耗降低81.5%，吞吐量提升2.13倍；在ResNet-20/CIFAR-10任务中，精度仅下降2.82个百分点，且FPGA端能效高达58.4 GOPS/W。

Conclusion: SPARX 框架通过结合近似计算、差分隐私、认证机制与定制化RISC-V架构，有效地解决了边缘AI在能源、性能、安全与隐私方面的多重限制，证明了其在高效嵌入式CNN推理中的可行性与卓越性能。

Abstract: Edge-AI systems increasingly require real-time CNN inference under strict energy, performance, security, and privacy constraints. Approximate computing improves hardware efficiency by exploiting the error resilience of neural network workloads; however, most approximate CNN accelerators do not jointly consider secure, privacy-aware edge deployment. This paper presents SPARX, a Secure and Privacy-Aware Approximate CNN Acceleration framework integrated within a heterogeneous RV32IMC RISC-V System-on-Chip (SoC). SPARX combines a custom RISC-V instruction extension, an approximate logarithmic CNN acceleration unit, a lightweight differential-noise-based privacy engine, and a challenge-response authentication mechanism. To guide arithmetic selection, an approximation-aware decision framework is introduced that uses the Approximation Severity Index (ASI), Approximation Efficiency (AE), Quality of Approximation (QoA), Approximation Figure-of-Merit (AFOM), and Hardware Acceleration Efficiency (HAE). Evaluation across 11 state-of-the-art approximate MAC architectures identifies the Iterative Logarithmic Multiplier (ILM) as the most suitable design, achieving 51.7% area reduction, 81.5% power reduction, and 2.13x throughput improvement compared with an accurate radix-4 Booth MAC, while only reducing ResNet-20/CIFAR-10 accuracy by 2.82 percentage points. FPGA implementation on a Xilinx VC707 platform achieves 58.4 GOPS/W energy efficiency at 250 MHz, while 28-nm CMOS physical implementation validates ASIC feasibility

</details>


### [145] [Toward Intelligent Prefetching: A Survey on Complex Memory Access Prediction Techniques](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09955)
*Sheel Sindhu Manohar*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文对数据预取技术进行了全面综述，特别关注了机器学习预取器。作者提出了一套针对局部性、实现架构和学习范式的分类框架，并分析了ML预取器在现代计算负载下的性能效能，提出了分层集成架构设计以平衡准确度、开销与运行时自适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于启发式（如下一行、步长、相关性）的预取技术在处理现代工作负载（如图形分析、稀疏矩阵、复杂指针操作）中存在的不规则、数据依赖访问模式时表现力不足。因此，需要综述现有技术，特别是机器学习驱动的预取器，以应对这一挑战。

Method: 采用PRISMA（系统评价和荟萃分析优先报告条目）方法论，对各类数据预取技术进行了系统性的文献综述与分类。建立了涵盖局部性类型、实现层级及机器学习范式（针对ML预取器）的三维分类法，并对ML预取器进行了多维度对比分析。

Result: 通过多维度对比分析，明确了机器学习预取器在存储开销、准确度、推理延迟、硬件可行性及泛化能力方面的表现，识别出研究中的关键矛盾与优化方向。

Conclusion: 本文指出了机器学习预取器在准确性与开销之间的帕累托前沿、模型复杂度与缓存层级的架构映射关系，以及运行时适应性与模型容量之间的本质矛盾，并倡导采用分层集成架构来解决这些问题。

Abstract: Data prefetching is a critical technique for bridging the processor-memory performance gap by predicting future memory accesses and retrieving data into on-chip caches before demand. While traditional prefetchers based on next-line, stride, and correlation heuristics perform well for regular access patterns, they are fundamentally inadequate for the irregular, data-dependent patterns prevalent in modern workloads such as graph analytics, sparse matrix computations, and pointer-intensive applications. This survey presents a systematic review of papers using a PRISMA-guided selection methodology. We propose a structured taxonomy that organizes prefetching techniques across three dimensions: locality type, including spatial and temporal locality; implementation layer, including hardware, software, and hybrid approaches; and, for the increasingly important class of ML-based prefetchers, learning paradigm, including supervised, reinforcement, and unsupervised learning, paired with training mode, including online and offline training. Through a multi-dimensional comparative analysis of ML-based prefetchers evaluated across storage overhead, accuracy, inference latency, hardware feasibility, and generalization ability, we identify three key findings: an accuracy-overhead Pareto frontier defined by model class, a natural architectural mapping between model complexity and cache hierarchy level, and a fundamental tension between runtime adaptability and model capacity that motivates hierarchical ensemble architectures.

</details>


### [146] [A Generic Modulo-$(2^n\pmδ)$ RNS Multiplier Based on Twit Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09965)
*Saeid Gorgin,Amirhossein Sadr,Behzad Salami,Dara Rahmati*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对 RNS 系统中 $2^n \pm \delta$ 模数的通用 twit-based 乘法器，通过优化算术流程显著提升了电路的延迟、面积和功耗性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模乘法是 RNS 数据路径中的计算瓶颈。尽管近期已有支持 $2^n \pm \delta$ 模数的 twit-based 表示法用于加减法，但尚缺乏兼容的、高效的通用模乘法器。本文旨在弥补这一空白，以提升加密、信号处理及机器学习加速器的性能。

Method: 提出了一种基于 twit 表示法的模 $(2^n \pm \delta)$ 乘法器。核心技术包括：操作数拆分（operand splitting）、模部分积生成、进位保留累加（carry-save accumulation）、溢出折叠（overflow folding）以及 twit 兼容的最终模加法。该设计通过将进位传播推迟到最后阶段，避免了传统先乘后约简设计中的长关键路径。

Result: 在 FreePDK 45nm 工艺下进行综合评估，结果显示与基准设计相比，该乘法器平均延迟降低了 20.5%，面积减少了 13.2%，功耗降低了 28.0%。系统级研究进一步验证了其在不同负载下可实现更低的端到端延迟。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一种用于残数系统（RNS）的高效通用 twit-based 模 $(2^n \pm \delta)$ 乘法器架构。该设计通过优化运算流程显著降低了关键路径延迟，并在 45nm 工艺下验证了其在延迟、面积和功耗方面的显著优势，证实了其在高性能算术逻辑电路中的实用价值。

Abstract: Modular multiplication is a fundamental arithmetic primitive in Residue Number Systems (RNS) and is often the dominant source of delay, area, and energy consumption in RNS datapaths used in cryptography, signal processing, and machine-learning accelerators. Recent work introduced a twit-based residue representation for moduli of the form $2^n \pm δ$, with $0 \le δ\le 2^{n-1}-1$, and showed that it enables efficient generic modular addition and subtraction across the full admissible $δ$ range. However, an efficient modular multiplier compatible with the same representation has remained unavailable. This paper presents a generic twit-based modulo-$(2^n \pm δ)$ multiplier for RNS channels. The proposed architecture computes the product through operand splitting, modular partial-product generation, carry-save accumulation, overflow folding, and a twit-compatible final modular addition. By deferring carry propagation to the final stage, the resulting organization avoids the long critical paths characteristic of conventional multiply-then-reduce designs. To demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed approach, we study a modulus set with 5-bit residue channels and show that, owing to the broad admissible range of $δ$, it can provide a sufficiently wide dynamic range. Moreover, additional 8-bit and 11-bit configurations are used to evaluate the proposed approach at larger channel widths. We implement and synthesize the proposed multiplier in a FreePDK 45\,nm flow, and the results show average reductions of 20.5\% in delay, 13.2\% in area, and 28.0\% in power relative to baseline designs. A system-level study further indicates that these circuit-level improvements translate into lower end-to-end latency over a broad range of modular multiplication and addition workloads.

</details>


### [147] [Fault Characterization and Hardening of Combinational Standard Cells Using 3D-TCAD Simulations for Cyber-Physical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10015)
*Ali Zarei,Amir M. Hajisadeghi,Hamid R. Zarandi*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于3D-TCAD模拟的CPS标准单元故障特性分析方法，并开发出一种高鲁棒性的加固型NAND单元，有效提升了系统的容错性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着网络物理系统（CPS）在各类关键任务中的广泛应用，技术节点的微缩使得数字系统组件对故障的敏感度增加，因此提升标准单元的容错性与可靠性显得尤为重要。

Method: 利用3D-TCAD（技术计算机辅助设计）模拟技术，对通用组合标准单元在不同故障能量、粒子入射角度及相邻单元效应下的故障敏感区域进行深入表征分析，并据此设计出加固型通用NAND逻辑单元。

Result: 成功识别了标准单元在多种场景下的故障敏感区，并提出的加固型NAND单元在模拟测试中表现出显著的抗粒子诱发故障的能力提升。

Conclusion: 通过对标准单元进行高精度故障表征，并针对性地进行加固设计，能够有效提升网络物理系统（CPS）底层逻辑元件的抗辐射及抗故障能力，增强系统的整体可靠性。

Abstract: Cyber-physical systems (CPSs) are increasingly employed in applications with various levels of mission criticality, making the reliability of digital system components essential for maintaining service quality. On the other hand, advancements in technology nodes have heightened reliability concerns in these systems. This paper presents a method for characterizing and enhancing the fault tolerance of combinational standard cells using 3D-TCAD simulations. Through detailed simulations, we identify fault-sensitive regions in widely used standard cells under diverse scenarios that include variations in fault energy, particle angle, and the adjacency effect of identical and non-identical neighboring cells. Following this high-precision characterization, a hardened version of a universal logic NAND cell is proposed that mitigates its vulnerabilities. Simulation results demonstrate substantial improvements in resilience to particle-induced faults.

</details>


### [148] [Isolation-aware Scheduling Framework for DNN-based End-to-End Autonomous Driving System on Tile-based Accelerators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10303)
*Chenguang Zhang,Yuanpeng Zhang,Chenhao Xue,Yihan Yin,Chen Zhang,Guangyu Sun*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: ADS-Tile 是一种针对自动驾驶异构 DNN 任务的调度器，通过权衡并行度和资源重配置开销，在 Tile 架构的硬件上实现了高资源效率与严格的延迟保障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: L4+ 自动驾驶系统需在严格延迟限制下运行多个异构深度神经网络（DNN），但现有方案要么因资源隔离导致资源浪费，要么因频繁触发昂贵的权重重新分配（重配置）导致延迟抖动及性能损耗。

Method: 提出了 ADS-Tile 系统，引入了“时空隔离共享空间（spatio-temporal isolation-sharing space）”概念，结合概率延迟模型和 DAG 感知的运行时调度器，在限制重配置开销的前提下，动态优化任务的放置（colocation）和并行度（DoP）。

Result: 在行业与学术基准测试中，ADS-Tile 在满足截止期限的前提下，比传统的工作保留调度器减少了高达 32% 的 Tile 使用量，并将由重配置引起的无效计算开销从 17%-44% 降低至 1.2% 以下。

Conclusion: 通过可控的时空共享机制，ADS-Tile 显著提升了基于 Tile 架构的自动驾驶系统的资源利用率，并保障了端到端任务的延迟可预测性。

Abstract: Level-4+ autonomous driving systems (ADS) must run dozens of heterogeneous deep neural networks (DNNs) as end-to-end (E2E) pipelines under a strict latency constraint (<=100 ms), even as execution time varies by up to 3.3x. Cost rules out dedicating isolated hardware to each function in mass-produced ADS, so these DNNs must be densely colocated on a single chip, which introduces shared-resource contention. Tile-based accelerators expose two scheduling opportunities that conventional ADS schedulers do not exploit. First, they provide a tunable degree of parallelism (DoP): assigning more tiles raises DoP and can shorten DNN execution time. Second, they provide hardware-native isolation: tiles can be physically partitioned among co-located DNNs. But using this flexibility is expensive: changing a task's DoP triggers a stop-migrate-restart reallocation of its weights and intermediate features. At ADS task rates of 10-240 Hz, these stalls accumulate along E2E chains and threaten deadlines. Reservation-based schedulers fix DoP and leave this flexibility unused; work-conserving schedulers exploit it but assume reallocation is cheap and treat deadlines as independent. We present ADS-Tile that combines configurable isolation and elastic reservation into a spatio-temporal isolation-sharing space that bounds where and when reallocation occurs; a probabilistic latency model and a DAG-aware runtime scheduler then use this space to decide task colocation and DoP under shared E2E deadlines. On an industry- and academia- derived ADS benchmark, ADS-Tile uses up to 32% fewer tiles than the work-conserving baseline in deadline-critical settings and cuts reallocation-induced wasted processing capacity from 17%-44% to below 1.2%. Controlled spatio-temporal sharing improves resource efficiency and latency predictability for tile-based ADS.

</details>


### [149] [A 185 TOPS/W/mm2 Bayesian Inference Engine with 640 aJ Write-Free FeFET GRNG for Uncertainty-Aware Aerial Search and Rescue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10822)
*Zephan M. Enciso,Xuezhong Niu,Xingtian Wang,Mohammad Mehdi Sharifi,Subhasish Mukherjee,Likai Pei,Halid Mulaosmanovic,Stefan Duenkel,Sven Beyer,Michael Niemier,Kai Ni,Ningyuan Cao*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文设计了一种基于FeFET的低功耗、高能效存内计算架构，通过零写入开销的高斯随机数生成器实现了高效的传感器数据贝叶斯推理，显著改善了边缘设备（如无人机）在搜救任务中的目标检测精度与能效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的确定性深度学习模型在搜救任务中易产生过度自信的误报，导致无人机进行低效的验证动作；而现有的贝叶斯神经网络（BNN）因采样计算量大，难以在电池受限的边缘平台上部署。

Method: 提出了一种基于铁电场效应管（FeFET）的贝叶斯推理引擎，核心包括一个嵌入在存内计算（CIM）宏中的无需写入的中心极限定理高斯随机数生成器（CLT-GRNG）。该架构通过对随机选择的已编程FeFET子集电流求和来生成高斯采样，规避了高能耗的重写操作。

Result: CLT-GRNG单元每个样本功耗仅为640 aJ，能效比提升560倍；CIM模块达到185 TOPS/W/mm²的性能，在搜救任务中有效提升了不确定性校准和对环境噪声的鲁棒性，减少了不必要的低置信度验证成本。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于FeFET的贝叶斯推理引擎，通过创新的电路设计实现了极高的能效比和不确定性感知能力，为资源受限的自主搜救无人机系统提供了一种可靠且高效的边缘AI解决方案。

Abstract: Aerial search and rescue missions require fast and reliable victim detection under uncertain and rapidly changing environments. Deterministic deep learning models can produce overconfident false positives, forcing unmanned aircraft systems to perform costly verification maneuvers that reduce search coverage and increase rescue delay. Bayesian neural networks provide uncertainty-aware detection, but their sampling overhead is challenging for battery-constrained edge platforms. This work presents a FeFET-based Bayesian inference engine with a write-free central limit theorem Gaussian random number generator embedded in a compute-in-memory macro. By summing currents from a randomly selected subset of minimum-sized, programmed-once FeFETs, the proposed architecture eliminates energy- and endurance-intensive write operations during inference while maintaining scalable Gaussian sampling. The CLT-GRNG consumes 640 aJ per sample, providing a 560x energy-efficiency improvement over prior BNN accelerators, while the CIM tile achieves 185 TOPS/W/mm2. Evaluated on aerial search and rescue detection, the Bayesian model improves uncertainty calibration and robustness under environmental corruption, reducing risk and enabling low-confidence detections to be filtered before costly verification. These results demonstrate an energy-efficient and uncertainty-aware edge AI engine for autonomous search and rescue systems.

</details>


### [150] [Arithmetic Packing on Wide Integer Datapaths in DSP Primitives of Modern FPGA Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11065)
*Titus Bornträger,Shane Fleming,Philipp Holzinger,Dietmar Fey,Michaela Blott,Thomas B. Preußer*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用FPGA DSP内部预加法器实现动态数据打包的新方法，显著提高了低精度神经网络在FPGA上的算力利用率和资源效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FPGA的DSP切片具有固定的数据通路宽度，在处理低精度神经网络量化时利用率极低，且现有的打包方案缺乏通用性或需要额外的资源开销。

Method: 利用DSP内部的预加法器（pre-adder），实现多个不同位宽（有符号/无符号）数据在单一DSP数据通路中的动态打包；并针对矩阵向量乘法和卷积运算分别设计了两种专用架构，集成于AMD FINN框架。

Result: 在UltraNet模型上实现，相比FINN参考架构，LUT资源利用率降低了21%，每DSP的帧率（FPS/DSP）提升了36%。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于DSP预加法器（pre-adder）的动态打包技术，能够有效提升异构位宽运算在FPGA DSP切片上的利用率，为低精度神经网络量化部署提供了高效的硬件架构方案。

Abstract: Deep Neural Networks increasingly employ low-precision quantization to reduce computational requirements. While FPGAs are well suited for workloads with heterogeneous precisions, their dedicated digital signal processing (DSP) slices only feature fixed-width datapaths that are significantly underutilized by low-bitwidth arithmetic. While previous approaches have already introduced the packing of multiple values onto the same wide DSP datapath, they either only support specific fixed bitwidths or are wasteful regarding the use of additional support logic external to the DSP. This paper proposes an efficient method to dynamically pack multiple (un-)signed inputs with arbitrary bitwidths into a wide multiplier path by leveraging the DSP's internal pre-adder. Building on this, we present two distinct architectures, one optimized for matrix-vector multiplications and the other for convolutions. Our implementations are integrated into AMD's FINN framework. With these optimizations, we reduce the LUT utilization by 21% and increase the FPS/DSP by 36% for the UltraNet model compared to the FINN reference.

</details>


### [151] [Coset Ensemble Decoder for Quantum Error Correction with Algorithm-Hardware Co-Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11076)
*Shuang Liang,Jubo Xu,Giulio Bassanino,Qianzhou Wang,Yidong Zhou,Yuncheng Lu,Zhiwen Mo,Paul H. J. Kelly,Bo Yuan,Wayne Luk,Hongxiang Fan*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对量子纠错的软硬件协同解码方法，通过陪集集成解码算法和时间复用的领域专用架构，在大幅降低硬件资源占用的同时，实现了优异的速度与准确性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子纠错（QEC）解码器在处理大规模量子计算时，难以在逻辑准确性与超低延迟之间实现优化平衡，且现有的空间架构资源扩展性较差。

Method: 1. 算法层面：提出“陪集集成解码”（Coset Ensemble Decoding），结合反向消除和无损图压缩技术。2. 硬件层面：设计领域专用架构（DSA），通过时间复用减小资源占用，并利用多库内存散列和分层ID映射优化并发访问。

Result: 相较于传统的MWPM和UF解码器，该方法在电路级去极化噪声模型下改善了准确性-延迟权衡，并将FPGA查找表（LUT）资源消耗降低了最高8.2倍。

Conclusion: 提出了一种协同设计方法，在保持高逻辑准确性的同时，显著降低了解码器的延迟和资源消耗，克服了现有解码算法（MWPM/UF）在扩展性上的瓶颈，且具备灵活的性能可调性。

Abstract: Reliable large-scale quantum computation relies on fault-tolerant architectures, where quantum error correction (QEC) continuously extracts and decodes error syndromes in real time. A critical component in QEC is the decoder, a classical subsystem that must simultaneously deliver high logical accuracy and ultra-low latency. This paper presents a novel algorithm-hardware co-design that improves the accuracy-latency trade-off over existing approaches such as vanilla Minimum-Weight Perfect Matching (MWPM) and Union-Find (UF) decoders. At the algorithmic level, we introduce coset ensemble decoding, which improves UF decoding by explicitly exploiting logically equivalent cosets. Our method performs ensemble forest exploration to generate multiple coset-consistent candidates and aggregates them to approximate coset-level maximum-likelihood decoding. We further reduce computational and memory complexity via reverse-order elimination and lossless graph compression, without sacrificing accuracy. At the hardware level, we design a domain-specific architecture that temporally reuses resources, avoiding the code-distance-proportional resource growth in prior spatial architectures. Several optimizations, such as multi-bank memory hashing and hierarchical ID mapping, are proposed to mitigate pipeline stalls and memory conflicts under highly concurrent access patterns. Under a circuit-level depolarizing noise model, our co-design approach achieves a better accuracy-latency trade-off than prior MWPM- and UF-based decoders, while reducing FPGA LUT consumption by up to 8.2 times compared with reported UF-based decoder resources. The tunable candidate number further exposes a flexible design knob, enabling users to tailor decoding performance to the requirements of different fault-tolerant workloads. Our implementation is publicly available at https://github.com/IMSeonL/coset-ensemble-decoder.

</details>


### [152] [Towards Autonomous Accelerator Design: FPGA Accelerator Generation with SECDA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11117)
*Vinamra Sharma,Xingjian Fu,Jude Haris,José Cano*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 该论文提出了SECDA-DSE框架，通过引入大语言模型（LLM）实现FPGA加速器设计空间探索的自动化，成功实现了从架构设计到FPGA硬件验证的全流程优化，显著降低了人工设计成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FPGA加速器设计空间极其复杂，当前的自动化方法仍高度依赖设计人员的领域知识，导致设计过程极其耗时且效率低下。

Method: 提出SECDA-DSE框架，通过集成LLM与SECDA生态系统，利用RAG和思维链（CoT）进行推理引导的探索，并结合结构化DSE生成器与反馈循环实现架构的迭代演进。

Result: 成功生成包括向量乘法、2D卷积和矩阵转置在内的三种加速器设计；所有设计均通过了FPGA的合成与硬件在环执行验证，且能有效捕捉不同计算任务的算力与数据吞吐权衡。

Conclusion: LLM赋能的设计空间探索（DSE）能够有效自动化FPGA加速器设计，在减少对领域专家依赖的同时，能够根据不同工作负载特征实现架构参数的自适应优化。

Abstract: Designing FPGA-based accelerators for modern artificial intelligence workloads requires exploring a large and complex hardware design space that involves architectural parameters, data flow strategies, and memory hierarchies, making the process very time consuming. While existing methodologies such as SECDA enable rapid hardware-software co-design through SystemC simulation and FPGA execution, identifying efficient accelerator configurations remains a largely manual process requiring extensive domain knowledge. SECDA-DSE is a framework that integrates Large Language Models (LLMs) into the SECDA ecosystem to guide design space exploration (DSE) of FPGA-based accelerators. It combines a structured DSE Explorer for generating candidate architectures with an LLM Stack that performs reasoning-guided exploration using retrieval-augmented generation and chain-of-thought prompting, coupled with a feedback loop for iterative and reinforced refinement. Building on our previous work introducing SECDA-DSE, this paper extends its evaluation by generating three accelerator designs, including element-wise vector multiplication, 2D convolution, and matrix transpose, and performing end-to-end execution on FPGA hardware. The results show that SECDA-DSE can generate SECDA-compliant accelerator designs that are successfully synthesized and executed on FPGA hardware. Furthermore, the generated designs capture kernel-specific trade-offs between compute parallelism and data movement, highlighting the potential of LLM-guided exploration to adapt architectural configurations across diverse workloads while reducing exploration time and the need for extensive human expertise.

</details>


### [153] [Defeat the Heap: Zero-Copy Data Movement in AXI4MLIR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11158)
*Elam Cohavi,Nicolas Bohm Agostini,Jude Haris,Antonino Tumeo,David Kaeli,José Cano*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了针对AXI4MLIR框架的零拷贝数据传输优化，通过直接在DMA映射内存中分配缓冲区，消除了冗余的CPU-加速器数据拷贝开销，显著提升了系统性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AXI4MLIR在主机与硬件加速器之间传输数据时，存在将堆内存数据复制到DMA映射内存的冗余暂存操作，导致了显著的运行时开销。

Method: 扩展现有的AXI4MLIR编译器框架及其accel方言，实现直接在DMA映射内存中进行缓冲区分配的低层转换（lowering）支持，从而实现零拷贝数据传输。

Result: 在矩阵乘法加速器测试中，该优化方案将主内存数据移动量减少了高达2倍，并提高了硬件加速器的整体利用率。

Conclusion: 通过直接在DMA映射内存中分配缓冲区，成功消除了冗余的数据暂存拷贝操作。该方案显著降低了内存带宽需求，提升了加速器的利用率。

Abstract: As custom hardware accelerators become increasingly central to machine learning workloads, efficient data transfer is critical for maximizing accelerator performance on linear algebra kernels. AXI4MLIR, an extension of the Multi-Level Intermediate Representation (MLIR) compiler framework for automated generation of host-accelerator driver code, incurs significant runtime overhead due to non-zero-copy CPU-accelerator data movement. During transfers from the host to the accelerator, data is copied from heap-allocated memory buffers into contiguous Direct Memory Access (DMA)-mapped buffers. This work identifies this copy as a redundant staging operation and eliminates it through zero-copy data movement. The optimization extends accel, an MLIR dialect introduced by AXI4MLIR, and implements lowering support that allocates buffers directly within DMA-mapped memory, thereby omitting the staging copy. We evaluate the proposed scheme using a configurable matrix-matrix multiplication accelerator and show that the zero-copy optimization reduces main memory data movement by up to 2x, increasing overall accelerator utilization.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [154] [Hardware-accelerated Aggregation: Unification and Specialization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10030)
*Alireza Shateri,Hongshi Tan,Michael Ng,Bingsheng He,Qizhen Zhang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文探讨了数据分析中聚合操作的硬件加速，提出一种兼顾可移植性与性能的统一开发框架，并量化评估了CPU、GPU和FPGA在性能和能效上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决数据分析系统中关键操作（聚合）在异构硬件（CPU、GPU、FPGA）上的性能与能效挑战，平衡编程便捷性（可移植性）与硬件执行效率之间的矛盾。

Method: 采用统一的硬件加速框架进行基准实现，并针对CPU、GPU和FPGA三种硬件平台进行特定的底层架构优化，对比评估了端到端及片上处理的性能与能效。

Result: 不仅提供了跨平台的聚合操作实现方案，还通过实验量化了不同架构下聚合操作的性能差异与能效表现，为数据分析加速器的选择提供了参考。

Conclusion: 通过在CPU、GPU和FPGA三种不同架构上实现与优化聚合操作，研究证明了通过通用框架实现可移植性是可行的，但要达到最优性能，仍需针对特定硬件进行深入优化。

Abstract: The high efficiency of domain-specific hardware has sparked substantial interest in adopting accelerators in data analytics systems. Among many choices, GPUs and FPGAs thrived as two popular solutions due to their prevalent deployments in cloud data centers. This paper investigates hardware acceleration solutions for aggregation, a critical data analytics operation. Specifically, we implement aggregation with a unified hardware acceleration framework, which trades efficiency for ease of programming and portability, and then further develop hardware-specific optimizations. We evaluate these solutions on three recent computing hardware platforms: a CPU, a GPU, and an FPGA, with metrics that cover both the performance and energy consumption of on-device and end-to-end processing.

</details>


### [155] [RATrain: A Resource-Aware Training Runtime for Large Language Models on Bandwidth-Constrained Heterogeneous Supercomputing Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10415)
*Yao Lu,Shiqing Ma,Zhongzhi Luan,Gen Li,Jiaxing Qi,Bin Han,Hailong Yang,Depei Qian*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: RATrain 是一款专为带宽受限、内存结构特殊的异构超算平台（MT-3000）设计的 LLM 训练运行时，通过精细化的资源感知调度大幅提升了训练效率和扩展性，并保持了模型训练的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 LLM 训练运行时高度依赖高带宽显存、快速互联和成熟的通信库，难以适应 MT-3000 这种具备显式内存层次结构、有限的可用 DDR 容量以及受限的集群间通信的异构超算平台。

Method: 1. 将非交错 1F1B 训练建模为训练状态生命周期调度问题；
2. 在层级和阶段局部粒度上调度梯度同步、参数更新、参数视图预取和激活重计算；
3. 开发针对 MT-3000 的执行后端，优化 FP16 GEMM、Attention Backward 和显存移动；
4. 引入资源感知规划器，在 20GB DDR 显存限制下优化训练配置。

Result: 1. 相比 MT-3000 适配后的类 GPU 训练策略，端到端加速比提升高达 1.35 倍；
2. LLaMA-2-7B 模型在 1024 个计算集群上扩展效果良好，吞吐量达 112,790.55 tokens/s，扩展效率为 97.0%；
3. 正确性验证表明，RATrain 与基准 1F1B 运行相比，损失轨迹基本一致，最大相对偏差仅为 0.081%。

Conclusion: RATrain 能够有效地在资源受限的异构超级计算机（MT-3000）上进行大规模 LLM 训练，在确保模型收敛正确性的同时，显著提升了训练速度和扩展效率，解决了传统 GPU 优化方案在特定硬件架构下无法直接适用的难题。

Abstract: Production heterogeneous supercomputing platforms are increasingly used to host large language model (LLM) training workloads. However, existing GPU-oriented training runtimes typically rely on high-bandwidth device memory, fast interconnects, and mature collective communication libraries, making them difficult to directly adapt to MT-3000, a platform with an explicit memory hierarchy, limited usable DDR capacity, and constrained inter-cluster communication. This paper presents RATrain, a resource-aware training runtime for dense LLMs on bandwidth-constrained heterogeneous supercomputing platforms. RATrain formulates standard non-interleaved 1F1B training as a training-state lifecycle scheduling problem, and schedules gradient synchronization, parameter update, parameter-view prefetching, and activation recovery at layer-level and stage-local granularity. RATrain further combines an MT-3000-aware execution backend for efficient and predictable FP16 GEMM, Attention Backward, and explicit data movement with a resource-aware planner that selects feasible training configurations under the 20GB usable-DDR constraint per compute cluster. We implement RATrain on a real MT-3000 platform and evaluate it using LLaMA-2-7B, Baichuan2-13B, Qwen2.5-32B, and LLaMA-2-70B configurations. Results show that RATrain achieves up to 1.35$\times$ end-to-end speedup over MT-3000-adapted GPU-style training strategies. For LLaMA-2-7B, RATrain scales to 1024 compute clusters, reaches 112,790.55 tokens/s, and achieves 97.0\% scaling efficiency. A further 1.028B-token correctness run shows that RATrain preserves the loss trajectory of a semantically equivalent Baseline-1F1B run, with a maximum relative loss deviation of 0.081\%.

</details>


### [156] [ASTRA-sim 3.0: Next-Level Distributed Machine Learning Simulations via High-Fidelity GPU and Infrastructure Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10440)
*William Won,Jinsun Yoo,Tuan Ta,Moumita Dey,Andy Balogh,Pradosh Datta,Furkan Eris,Conor Green,Winston Liu,Changhai Man,Kingshuk Mandal,Amos Rai,Vinay Ramakrishnaiah,Ruchi Shah,David Sidler,Harsh Sikhwal,Hanjiang Wu,Tushar Krishna,Bradford M. Beckmann*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: ASTRA-sim 3.0通过引入缓存行级执行建模和统一的基础设施表示（InfraGraph），实现了分布式ML系统的细粒度、高保真度模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着分布式ML推理需求的增加，现有的模拟器在捕捉设备架构、控制路径及数据路径方面缺乏足够的保真度，且缺乏统一的基础设施表示方法，限制了系统性能优化和设计空间探索。

Method: 提出以缓存行（cache-line）大小的加载-存储粒度进行模拟，整合了详细的GPU执行模型，并引入了标准化基础设施表示工具InfraGraph，平衡了模拟的可扩展性与高保真度。

Result: 更新后的ASTRA-sim 3.0能够实现更高保真度的协作通信模拟，并在优化协作算法、评估网络需求和GPU架构设计方面展示了有效的实验探索能力。

Conclusion: 通过引入细粒度模拟和标准化的InfraGraph表示，ASTRA-sim 3.0显著提升了在分布式机器学习基础设施中进行性能评估和架构设计的保真度与灵活性。

Abstract: Distributed machine learning (ML) is a key paradigm for today's large-scale artificial intelligence applications. As model inference arises as an important use case, faithful modeling of latency-sensitive collective communication has never been more important. Capturing the device architecture and modeling control and data paths at high fidelity is therefore a necessity today. Having a common, detailed representation for distributed ML infrastructure is also crucial. We revisit the promising open-source, community-driven simulator: ASTRA-sim. In this work, we identify limitations of the current ASTRA-sim simulator and augment it with new features. To this end, we enable fine-grained, high-fidelity simulation with a standardized infrastructure representation, opening new design space exploration opportunities. We propose the simulation at cache-line-sized load-store granularity, with a detailed graphics processing unit (GPU) execution model, to balance simulation scalability and fidelity. We also introduce InfraGraph, a standardized representation to capture distributed ML network infrastructure in detail. Using the updated ASTRA-sim 3.0 simulator, we showcase interesting design space explorations for designing optimized collective algorithms, network requirements, and GPU architectures.

</details>


### [157] [Achieving Cloud-Grade SLOs for Local Mixture-of-Experts Inference through CPU-GPU Hybrid Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10493)
*Wenxin Wang,Yule Hou,Yu Ji,Peng Qu,Youhui Zhang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对本地部署大型MoE模型性能低效的问题，该论文提出了一种CPU-GPU混合推理系统，通过流式加载、专家并行、内存池重叠及CPU指令加速等技术，在消费级硬件上实现了高吞吐、低延迟的云级MoE模型推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本地部署大型MoE模型在服务质量上面临显著不足，包括模型被迫裁剪（量化、蒸馏等）、长上下文处理速度慢（TTFT过高）、解码吞吐量低以及混合负载下的并发能力差。现有的本地推理方案无法达到类云端服务水平（SLOs）。

Method: 提出了一套CPU-GPU混合推理系统，包括：(1) Stream-loading prefill (SLP) 及分布式DSLP；(2) 基于SmallEP的专家并行机制；(3) 针对Prefill-Decode解耦的零拷贝权重与双批次注意力-MoE重叠调度；(4) AVX-512优化的FP8 GEMV内核以加速CPU计算；(5) 细粒度的CPU并行优化。

Result: 在RTX 5090等消费级硬件上实现了显著性能提升：预填充吞吐量达1,800 tokens/s（支持45K上下文快于30s）；FP8 V3模型解码速度达21.5 tokens/s；在保持模型完整精度的情况下，相比基准方案显著改善了并发性能。

Conclusion: 该系统证明了在消费级CPU-GPU硬件上部署未经裁剪的旗舰级MoE模型（如DeepSeek-V3）是可行的。通过软硬件协同优化，该系统不仅降低了本地部署的推理性能差距，还实现了无需数据中心基础设施即可获得云级QoS的高性价比部署方案。

Abstract: Local deployment of large Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models falls short of the service quality achieved in cloud-scale environments, even under low-concurrency workloads. We identify four key gaps in local MoE inference: reliance on capacity-reduced models (quantized, distilled, rerouted), inability to meet 30-second TTFT for long prefills (more than 12K), sub-baseline decode throughput (under 20 tokens/s), and poor concurrency under mixed prefill-decode and batched decode workloads. We present a CPU-GPU hybrid system that achieves cloud-level SLOs on dual-socket commodity CPUs and consumer GPUs by (1) stream-loading prefill (SLP), boosting prefill throughput to 1,200 tokens/s and enabling 32K prompts within 30 seconds; (2) distributed SLP (DSLP) with SmallEP expert parallelism, reaching 1,800 tokens/s and 45K prompts in 30 seconds on two RTX 5090s; (3) intra-node prefill-decode disaggregation with zero-copy shared weights and a dual-batch attention-MoE overlap scheme, sustaining concurrency with under 15 percent latency increase and 50 percent throughput gains; (4) an AVX-512-optimized FP8 GEMV kernel, enabling native CPU FP8 inference while delivering 4-5x lower CPU latency; and (5) fine-grained CPU parallelism that attains 28 tokens/s on INT4 DeepSeek-V3 and 21.5 tokens/s on intact FP8 V3. Evaluations show our system delivers cloud-level QoS for flagship MoE models on consumer CPU-GPU platforms, reshaping local deployment with intact, original-precision inference and enabling high-quality, cost-effective access without datacenter infrastructure.

</details>


### [158] [Generalizing LCL Complexity Gaps to Unbounded Degree via Monadic Second-Order Properties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10693)
*Chiara Piombi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文将局部可验证标签（LCL）问题推广至无界度数图，定义了 LPMSO 问题类，并证明了原先在有界度数树上的复杂性间隙和判定性定理在高阶无界度数有根树环境下依然有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的 LCL 问题定义局限于有界度数图，这限制了现有复杂性分类和间隙定理的适用性。研究旨在将这一模型推广到更广泛的无界度数图场景中。

Method: 引入了 Local PMSO (LPMSO) 问题类，将 LCL 问题的定义推广至无界度数图。LPMSO 问题被定义为：解的正确性可由 Presburger MSO (PMSO) 公式描述，且可由常量时间的 LOCAL 算法进行局部验证。研究利用该框架在无界度数树上重新审视了复杂性间隙。

Result: 证明了在无界度数有根树上，存在 $\omega(\log n)$ 到 $n^{o(1)}$ 以及 $\omega(n^{1/(k+1)})$ 到 $o(n^{1/k})$ 的复杂性间隙，并且这些间隙的判定性依然成立。

Conclusion: 作者证明了在无界度数有根树上，LOCAL 模型中关于 LCL 问题的复杂性间隙（gap）结果同样适用于 LPMSO 问题，拓展了这些分类定理的适用范围。

Abstract: The last decade of research on the LOCAL model has seen tremendous progress in understanding locally checkable labeling (LCL) problems, culminating in an almost complete classification of the possible complexities LCL problems can exhibit. In particular, on undirected trees, Chang and Pettie showed that there is no LCL problem with complexity between $ω(\log n)$ and $n^{o(1)}$ and Chang showed that, for every positive integer $k$, there is no LCL problem with complexity between $ω(n^{1/(k+1)})$ and $o(n^{1/k})$; additionally, which side of each gap a problem is found on is decidable.
  While the class of LCL problems - which, roughly speaking, consists of problems for which the correctness of a solution can be described by a finite set of allowed node configurations, which in turn can be locally verified by a constant-time algorithm - includes many important problems, it has one major restriction: problems can be defined only on bounded degree graphs, which consequently restricts all the classification and gap results mentioned above.
  In this work, we propose a generalization of LCL problems to unbounded degree using Presburger monadic second-order (PMSO) formulas; more specifically, we consider what we call Local PMSO (LPMSO) problems, i.e., problems whose correct solutions are both finitely described by a PMSO formula and locally verifiable by a LOCAL algorithm in constant time - this class contains many of the important problems studied in the LOCAL model but defines them on unbounded degree graphs. As our main result we prove that, on unbounded degree rooted trees, the aforementioned $ω(\log n)$ - $n^{o(1)}$ and $ω(n^{1/(k+1)})$ - $o(n^{1/k})$ complexity gaps (and their decidability) extend to the class of LPMSO problems.

</details>


### [159] [Dynamic Software Updates using CRDTs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10920)
*Seppe Wyns,Jim Bauwens,Elisa Gonzalez Boix*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本论文提出了一种名为App CRDT的模型，通过将应用程序代码作为CRDT进行复制和版本管理，配合中间件支持，实现了分布式系统的动态、平滑且通用的软件更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式应用在运行期间进行动态软件更新时，往往面临分布式节点之间代码版本不一致导致的可用性及一致性难题，现有方案难以处理广泛的分布式场景。

Method: 1. 提出一种新型“App CRDT”，用于存储与语义版本关联的应用程序代码，以定义代码更新的全序关系；2. 设计兼容API的消息中间件，支持在即使存在向后不兼容更新时，部分更新的组件仍能协同工作；3. 在AmbientTalk语言及LuAT（Lua框架）中进行了实现与验证。

Result: 该方法成功实现了分布式环境下的平滑动态代码更新，且在AmbientTalk和LuAT中的应用表明该方案具有极低的集成难度和良好的跨语言通用性。

Conclusion: 利用CRDT（冲突无关复制数据类型）可以有效地解决分布式系统中动态更新的代码一致性与可用性问题，证明了通过代码复制与语义版本控制实现平滑、持续更新的可行性及通用性。

Abstract: This paper investigates how Conflict-free Replicated Data Types (CRDTs) can be used for dynamic software updates of distributed applications. We propose to model application updates as a new App CRDT that stores the application code associated with a semantic version, which defines a total order of the code updates. The App CRDT works with an API-compatible message delivery middleware, which allows applications to continue working with partially updated components in the face of backwards-incompatible software updates. We implemented our approach in AmbientTalk, an ambient-oriented programming language designed for distributed systems. We show how this CRDT can be integrated with existing AmbientTalk applications, requiring minimal changes. We also implemented our approach in LuAT, an ambient-oriented programming framework for Lua. This shows that our approach of using CRDTs to replicate code can be generalised to other programming languages.

</details>


### [160] [FairWave : A Fairness-Aware Asynchronous DAG-BFT Consensus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10982)
*Syariful Mujaddiq*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: FairWave 是一个新型双通道 DAG-BFT 协议，通过解耦权益选择与奖励分配，在保证抗女巫攻击的同时，有效抑制了 PoS 共识协议中长期的中心化趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 DAG-BFT 协议（如 Bullshark 等）在追求活性（liveness）时牺牲了权益分配的公平性，导致网络长期运行中出现显著的中心化现象（ plutocracy）。

Method: 提出 FairWave 双通道 DAG BFT 协议，将锚定点选择与奖励分配分离：选择通道采用超线性权重（抑制女巫攻击），奖励通道采用平方根归一化权重（抑制富者愈富）；引入基于滞后信誉（lagged reputation）的机制避免循环依赖。

Result: 经 55 万轮 Monte Carlo 测试及 8 个基准测试对比，FairWave 实现了更低的 Gini 系数（0.149 vs 0.488）和单调递减的 HHI 指数；同时在安全方面保持了 $f < n/3$ 的安全性，并实现了优于传统协议的连续性活性降级曲线。

Conclusion: FairWave 创新性地提出了将共识选择与权益奖励解耦的机制，有效地平衡了抗女巫攻击与长期的去中心化目标，解决了传统 BFT 协议中存在的“富者愈富”导致的中心化难题。

Abstract: Combining asynchronous Byzantine Fault Tolerant (BFT) consensus with Proof-of-Stake (PoS) creates a trilemma between Sybil resistance, reward distribution fairness, and protection against persistent plutocracy. Existing DAG-BFT approaches (Narwhal+Tusk, Bullshark, and Mysticeti) prioritize liveness over the fairness implications of stake-based selection, resulting in persistent longitudinal centralization.FairWave is a dual-channel DAG BFT protocol that separates anchor selection from reward distribution. The selection channel is super-linear in stake, guaranteeing Sybil gain < 1 for all split factors K > 1. The reward channel is sub-linear, using square-root stake normalization to mitigate rich-get-richer dynamics.The finalized DAG structure provides deterministic uptime and latency factors, allowing honest validators to agree on operational quality without any external oracle. To avoid circular dependency between selection outcomes and selection weights, reputation is used in a lagged form: the active value at epoch e equals the prior epoch's final value. We derive closed-form constraints for both channels and validate them through nine empirical analyses (approximately 550,000 Monte Carlo rounds) against eight baselines. FairWave achieves a Gini coefficient of 0.149 (vs. Pure-PoS's 0.488), a monotone HHI reduction from 0.039 to 0.021 over 50,000 epochs, an optimal-adversary Sybil split of K* = 1, and a success-rate coefficient of variation of 5.2% under +/-25% input perturbation. Safety (agreement and validity) is a formal consequence of the 2f+1 strong-support commit rule, holding unconditionally for f < n/3; the empirical differential is the monotone-continuous liveness-degradation curve, which decreases from 99.6% commit rate at b=0.20 to 71.1% at the theoretical bound b=1/3 without the discontinuous cliff characteristic of view-change-driven leader-BFT.

</details>


### [161] [A Neurosymbolic Prolog Skill for LLM-Driven Service Placement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11113)
*Jacopo Massa,Giuseppe Bisicchia,Patrizio Dazzi,Antonio Brogi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对云边协同布局任务，提出了一种结合大型语言模型与Prolog的神经符号框架，利用Prolog处理形式化约束推理，解决了纯神经方法缺乏透明度和保证的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有云边协同布局方法在显式建模的优化效率与神经方法（缺乏透明度及形式化保证）之间存在权衡，缺乏既具备灵活性又能满足严格约束条件的解决方案。

Method: 引入“Prolog技能”（Prolog skill），将LLM的自然语言处理能力与Prolog的符号化推理能力结合。LLM负责解析任务意图并生成符号事实、规则与查询，Prolog负责执行布局意图的约束验证与逻辑推理。

Result: 该方法有效实现了将高级意图转化为可验证的布局决策，增强了云边资源分配的可解释性和受控性。

Conclusion: 提出了一种神经符号方法，通过将任务分配给Prolog进行验证和推理，弥合了大型语言模型（LLM）的灵活性与资源布局中严格约束条件之间的鸿沟，实现了可审计、策略感知的云边放置决策。

Abstract: Service placement in the cloud-edge continuum requires assigning application components to heterogeneous resources under multiple constraints, including latency, locality, and policy requirements. Existing approaches rely on optimisation models or heuristics that require explicit modelling, while neural methods lack transparency and formal guarantees. This work proposes a neuro-symbolic alternative based on a Prolog skill, a reusable interface for schema-constrained fact generation and querying, for constraint-aware placement. The skill enables a language model to structure placement intent into symbolic facts, rules, and queries, while delegating validation and reasoning to Prolog. This design bridges high-level intent and formal constraint evaluation, enabling inspectable and policy-aware placement decisions in cloud-edge environments.

</details>


### [162] [Revisiting "Cooler is Better": ITD-Aware Per-CPU Thermal Optimization for Sustainable Data Center Operation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11163)
*Jason Crop,Hayden Moore,Sudeep Pasricha*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 现代CPU存在“逆温度依赖性”（ITD），即一味降低温度反而会降低能效。该研究首次在生产环境中证实了这一点，并提出通过ITD感知的热管理策略，可为数据中心节能4%-13%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据中心普遍认为“温度越低越节能”，但在现代低压CPU中存在“逆温度依赖性”（ITD），使得较低温度下反而增加了供电电压，导致能效下降，传统冷却策略在能效优化上存在盲区。

Method: 通过在Amazon和Equinix等生产环境中的Intel Xeon服务器上进行实证测量，表征ITD效应；分析CPU的能效曲线，确定与CPU部件相关的最佳运行温度，并提出ITD感知的热分组及进气温度调节方案。

Result: 研究发现约一半的高功耗CPU实际运行温度比最优能效点低约10℃。通过实施ITD感知的冷却优化方案，数据中心可降低4%-13%的总能耗。

Conclusion: 数据中心应摒弃“越凉越好”的传统冷却策略，转而采用考虑逆温度依赖性（ITD）的温控策略。通过对CPU进行热分组并动态调整进气温度，可在保障性能的前提下显著提升能效。

Abstract: As data center energy demand approaches grid-level constraints, optimizing conventional server infrastructure is essential for sustainable growth. The long-standing assumption that "cooler is better", i.e., lower CPU temperatures reduce power, does not fully hold for modern low-voltage CPUs, where inverse temperature dependence (ITD) drives higher supply voltages at lower temperatures. This creates a non-monotonic performance-per-watt curve where efficiency peaks at an intermediate thermal point. In this paper, for the first time, we empirically characterize ITD on production Intel Xeon CPUs and demonstrate that efficiency-optimal temperatures are CPU part-specific, and frequently higher than typical data center operating conditions. Measurements from commercial cloud data center platforms (Amazon, Equinix) reveal that approximately half of modern high-power CPUs operate about 10°C below their efficiency-optimal thermal point. By implementing ITD-aware thermal grouping of CPUs and inlet temperature adjustments, data center operators can optimize facility-level cooling and overall sustainability. Our case study shows that this approach can reduce total data center energy by 4-13% without sacrificing performance or reliability.

</details>


### [163] [Piper: A Programmable Distributed Training System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11169)
*Megan Frisella,Shubham Tiwari,Andy Ruan,Yi Pan,Parker Gustafson,Mat Jacob,Gilbert Bernstein,Stephanie Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Piper 是一个分布式训练系统，通过引入 IR 抽象和策略解耦设计，使用户能够通过简单指令灵活定制复合并行策略，解决了现有框架难以快速适应新型分布式训练策略的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大规模模型训练面临并行策略复杂化（如数据、流水线、专家并行与 ZeRO 的混合）的挑战。现有的系统要么依赖人工手动设计实现（难以适配新策略），要么受限于固定的并行实现模式（灵活性不足，难以扩展新型策略）。

Method: Piper 提出了一种基于声明式的分布式训练系统：1. 使用抽象的中间表示（IR）统一描述全局计算和通信图（DAG）；2. 允许用户通过少量的模型注解和调度指令直接操作 IR；3. 编译器自动根据指令生成各设备的执行计划；4. 底层运行时完全与具体策略解耦，仅负责执行编排后的计算与通信任务。

Result: Piper 在传统并行策略（如 ZeRO）上表现出与现有系统相当的性能，并成功通过联合调度计算与通信，为新兴的复合并行策略（如 DeepSeek-V3 的 DualPipe）提供了比传统实现更好的性能与内存效率表现。

Conclusion: Piper 通过引入中间表示（IR）层，成功实现了并行策略与运行时实现的解耦。该方法不仅在处理传统并行策略时保持了性能，还能通过策略组合有效应对新兴模型架构（如 DeepSeek-V3 的 DualPipe），显著提升了大型模型训练的可扩展性和开发灵活性。

Abstract: Large-scale model training increasingly relies on composing multiple parallelism strategies, such as data, pipeline, and expert parallelism, together with memory-saving optimizations like ZeRO. Deployed systems for foundation model pretraining often rely on human experts to manually design a high-level parallelism strategy then implement the corresponding low-level execution strategy, making it difficult to adapt the system to new strategies. Meanwhile, many general-purpose frameworks are more flexible but their implementations are still tied to a fixed set of common parallelism strategies, making it challenging to integrate state-of-the-art strategies.
  We present Piper, a user-controllable distributed training system that decouples the strategy from the runtime implementation. Piper allows users to declare a comprehensive distributed training strategy with a small set of model annotations and scheduling directives. Each directive applies a transformation on Piper's intermediate representation (IR), a unified global training DAG that represents all computation and communication. Using this IR, Piper compiles per-device execution plans and executes them with a distributed runtime agnostic to the strategy. We show that the combined system maintains performance parity on commonly available strategies such as ZeRO, while also enabling additional performance and memory efficiency gains through joint scheduling of compute and communication in composed parallelism strategies such as DeepSeek-V3's DualPipe.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [164] [Convergence Rates for Neural-Network Estimation with Current-Status Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10119)
*Yuan Wu,Tianhui Zhou*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于ReLU神经网络的非参数最大似然估计方法，用于处理当前状态生存数据，并从理论上建立了其收敛速率，为其在统计分析中的应用提供了理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对当前状态数据（Current-status data，即事件时间仅通过检查时的发生指标观测得到）的非参数估计问题，旨在利用神经网络的逼近能力解决传统方法在处理此类数据时的局限性。

Method: 采用“神经网络筛最大似然估计法”（Neural-Network Sieve Maximum Likelihood Estimator），结合ReLU激活函数的神经网络逼近理论与经验过程论，推导了条件累积分布函数的收敛速率。

Result: 在Hölder光滑性假设下，建立了神经网络估计量的明确收敛速率，证明了该方法在处理当前状态数据时的理论可行性。

Conclusion: 本研究通过理论证明为使用神经网络估计当前状态数据提供了严谨的数学支持，明确了该方法在非参数统计推断中的有效性和收敛速度。

Abstract: Current-status data arise when an event time is observed only through an indicator of whether it occurred before an examination time. This paper studies a nonparametric neural-network sieve maximum likelihood estimator of the conditional cumulative distribution function of the event time. Under Hölder smoothness assumptions, we establish an explicit convergence rate by combining approximation theory for rectified linear unit neural networks with empirical-process arguments. This result provides theoretical support for neural-network estimation and subsequent inference under current-status observation.

</details>


### [165] [Robust Active Learning for Few-Shot Example Selection in Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10125)
*Arash Pourhabib*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对Text-to-SQL少样本学习的高效示例选择框架，通过分层贪婪算法优化异方差互信息，有效平衡了标注成本与检索精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Text-to-SQL系统中，高质量示例库对提升性能至关重要，但专家标注成本极高；当前缺乏针对异方差性、空间多样性约束和模型错误设定等挑战的有效示例选择方案。

Method: 将主动选择示例形式化为语义查询嵌入流形上的受限实验设计问题；提出了最大化异方差互信息目标的“分层贪婪算法”（Stratified Greedy Algorithm），并利用次模性与近似单调性提供了常数因子近似保证。

Result: 实验结果表明，该策略显著降低了标注工作量，同时保持了高准确率；理论分析证明了该算法在模型错误设定下的退化边界是可控的（稳健性）。

Conclusion: 本文提出的分层贪婪算法通过最大化异方差互信息，在解决Text-to-SQL少样本示例选择问题上实现了高效的标注成本降低，且具备鲁棒的理论性能保障。

Abstract: Few-shot example retrieval is the dominant paradigm for grounding large language models (LLMs) in domain-specific text-to-SQL systems. However, the quality of the annotated example bank directly governs system accuracy, and expert annotation is prohibitively expensive. We formalize the active selection of these examples as a constrained experimental design problem over the intrinsic, low-dimensional manifold of semantic query embeddings. Unlike standard active learning frameworks, our setting introduces three critical challenges: varying, query-dependent annotation reliability (heteroscedasticity), strict requirements for spatial diversity across semantic topics (partition matroid constraints), and the inherent reality that the true covariance structure of the embedding space is unknown (misspecification). To address these, we propose a stratified greedy algorithm that maximizes a heteroscedastic mutual information objective. We prove that this objective remains submodular and approximately monotonic on the intrinsic manifold, yielding a theoretical constant-factor approximation guarantee. We establish a spectral bound demonstrating that this approximation guarantee degrades gracefully, rather than catastrophically, when the assumed surrogate kernel diverges from the true underlying data-generating process. Empirical results demonstrate that the proposed strategy significantly reduces labeling effort while maintaining high text-to-SQL retrieval accuracy.

</details>


### [166] [Decision-Calibrated Conformal Uncertainty for Pacing Decisions in Streaming Advertising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10187)
*Prashant Shekhar,Caroline Howard*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种决策导向的一致性预测框架，通过聚焦于对决策有实质影响的误差，在广告投放中实现了比传统方法更精准、更少保守的不确定性控制和更高质量的性能保障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的广告投放（pacing）系统受限于未来库存、需求压力、响应增量及用户体验负载的高度不确定性。传统的通用残差校准过于保守，且未考虑不确定性对具体决策的影响。

Method: 提出一种决策校准一致性（decision-calibrated conformal）框架，将预测误差衡量标准定义为对实际可部署策略影响最大的部分，并使用分割一致性校准实现有限样本覆盖，通过支持函数来刻画策略敏感度集合。

Result: 在Criteo Uplift和KuaiRand数据集上的实验表明，相比传统方法高达数千的残差半径，该方法将不确定性半径分别显著降至18.4和278.6；在Criteo数据集上，将违规率从16.7%降低至3.3%，且实现了预算和负载违规归零。

Conclusion: 决策导向的校准比传统的残差校准更能有效降低广告投放中的不确定性，从而在避免过度保守的同时保证决策的安全性。预测、响应以及用户体验模型应根据其缩小决策不确定性的能力来评估。

Abstract: We develop a decision-calibrated conformal framework for pacing decisions in streaming advertising. Pacing depends on uncertain future inventory, demand pressure, incremental response, and member-experience load. Instead of calibrating a generic forecast residual, the framework measures forecast error by its largest impact on the policies that could actually be deployed. The main theorem shows that the proposed score is the smallest valid uncertainty measure that uniformly protects all deployable pacing policies. Geometrically, it is the support function of the signed policy sensitivity set. Split conformal calibration gives finite-sample coverage for this score. A high-dimensional separation theorem shows that traditional residual calibration can be arbitrarily more conservative by paying for nuisance inventory dimensions, and a robust pacing result combines inventory, response, and experience uncertainty. On public-data-calibrated pacing replays built from Criteo Uplift and KuaiRand datasets, traditional conformal pacing remains unresolved with high residual radii of 7236.7 on Criteo and 4629.4 on KuaiRand. With the proposed decision calibration approach, the uncertainty radii are reduced to 18.4 and 278.6 respectively, with separate margins for value, delivery, budget, and member load. On Criteo, the proposed method certifies a less aggressive pacing policy than the point-forecast baseline, and reduces held-out any-violation rate from 16.7% to 3.3%, with zero budget and member-load violations. On KuaiRand, the choice remains unresolved. In a nutshell, the paper establishes that forecasts, response estimates, and member-experience models should be judged by whether they shrink the uncertainty that the pacing decision uses, as this leads to confident decisions that are not overly conservative.

</details>


### [167] [$k$-Nearest Neighbors in Gromov--Wasserstein Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10295)
*Kaitlyn Hohmeier,Nicolas Fraiman,Caroline Moosmueller*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文证明了基于 Gromov--Wasserstein (GW) 和 Fused GW 距离的 $k$-近邻分类器在图和节点属性图分类任务中具有通用一致性，并在实验中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gromov--Wasserstein 距离能有效处理不同节点规模及不同结构的图数据比较。为了验证该框架在图分类任务中的有效性，作者旨在从理论上证明基于 GW 和 fGW 的 $k$-NN 分类器在图数据上的通用一致性。

Method: 将图视为具有有限支撑和均匀概率测度的度量测度空间；利用 Gromov--Wasserstein (GW) 和 Fused Gromov--Wasserstein (fGW) 距离构建 $k$-近邻 ($k$-NN) 分类器；基于度量空间及结构化对象的通用一致性框架进行收敛性证明；并通过数值实验验证模型性能。

Result: 证明了 GW-$k$-NN 分类器在有限支撑的度量测度空间（及其对应的图）上的通用一致性；证明了 fGW-$k$-NN 在节点属性图上的通用一致性；数值实验表明该方法在多个图数据集上表现优异。

Conclusion: 本文通过理论证明建立了 GW-$k$-NN 和 fGW-$k$-NN 在度量测度空间及图数据分类中的通用一致性（Universal Consistency），为基于 GW 距离的图分类方法提供了坚实的理论支撑。

Abstract: The Gromov--Wasserstein (GW) distance provides a framework for comparing metric measure spaces, regardless of their underlying structure or geometry. For network-based data, it enables direct comparisons of graphs with different numbers of nodes, without requiring an embedding or other abstraction. Furthermore, through a variant of GW known as fused Gromov--Wasserstein (fGW), it is also possible to incorporate node features in addition to graph structure. In this work, we implement $k$-nearest neighbors ($k$-NN) classification using the GW and fGW distances. We prove the universal consistency of the GW-$k$-NN classifier on the space of equivalence classes of metric measure spaces with finite support and uniform probability measure. By viewing graphs as finitely supported metric measure spaces equipped with the pairwise distance metric and a uniform probability measure on the nodes, we obtain universal consistency of GW-$k$-NN for the space of graphs. Likewise for fGW-$k$-NN, we prove universal consistency on the space of weak isomorphism classes of structured objects consisting of metric measure spaces with finite support and uniform probability measure and feature maps into Euclidean space, thus establishing universal consistency on the space of node-attributed graphs. Our numerical experiments show that GW-$k$-NN and fGW-$k$-NN consistently perform well across multiple graph datasets, suggesting that metric classifiers such as $k$-NN work well in the GW framework.

</details>


### [168] [Near-Exponential Convergence Rates for kNN Classification based on Boltzmann Margin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10361)
*Luyuan Yang,Shayan Shafaei,Chao Lan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个新的“Boltzmann margin”条件，弥补了Tsybakov和Massart margin在收敛率分析上的空缺，并证明了kNN分类器在该条件下可达到近指数级的收敛速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Tsybakov margin条件较弱但收敛率仅为多项式级，Massart margin条件较强可实现指数级收敛，研究旨在填补两者之间的理论鸿沟。

Method: 引入“Boltzmann margin”条件，将其应用于kNN分类器的收敛性分析，并提供理论扩展及数值验证。

Result: 提出了Boltzmann margin；证明了在该条件下，kNN分类器具有近指数级的收敛率；通过数值实验验证了理论结果的有效性。

Conclusion: Boltzmann margin是一种灵活的条件，能够桥接Tsybakov与Massart margin，为分类器（特别是kNN）的收敛性分析提供了更精确的理论框架，实现了近指数级收敛率。

Abstract: Convergence-rate analysis for classifiers is often conducted under either Tsybakov margin or Massart margin. The former is a relatively weak condition that typically yields polynomial rates, while the latter is substantially stronger but can guarantee exponential rates. In this paper, we introduce a new condition, called Boltzmann margin, that bridges the gap between these two regimes. It is weaker than Massart margin, generally stronger than Tsybakov margin, and can imply many of their properties under suitable conditions. We apply Boltzmann margin to the analysis of kNN classifiers and establish the first near-exponential convergence rates for kNN classification. We also present extensions of the main results and provide numerical evidence supporting the main theoretical implications.

</details>


### [169] [Human-AI Teaming Through the Lens of Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10906)
*Eric Nalisnick,Chi Zhang,Sophia Qian,Yixin Wang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文探讨了人机协作中的统计校准问题，发现预测组合方法会破坏人类校准度，而基于委派的方法则对决策机制提出了过高的校准需求，特别是在人类使用隐含信息决策时，协作效能面临严峻挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探究人类与AI在协作中，各自的预测校准度如何受到协作框架（预测组合或任务委派）的影响，并揭示现有框架在保持人机预测有效性方面的局限性。

Method: 本文通过统计校准（statistical calibration）的视角分析了人机协作框架，主要涉及两种模式：(1) 将人类与AI的预测进行组合；(2) 将预测任务委派给人类或AI。通过理论推导和实验验证来分析校准假设在这些框架中的传播与影响。

Result: 研究表明，常见的组合预测算法会损害人类原本的校准程度；委派型框架虽然逻辑上可行，但将压力转移到了预测决策者（rejector）身上，且当人类依赖非观测特征进行决策时，实现高精度的委托校准是不切实际的。

Conclusion: 人机协作中的预测组合方法无法保持人类的校准度；委派机制虽然能保持后续预测器的校准，但极度依赖“委托人（rejector）”的精确校准。当人类依赖系统无法观测的信息时，这种精确校准在理论上无法实现。

Abstract: We study models for human-AI teaming through the lens of statistical calibration. We assume the team consists of an AI model and human -- both of which are calibrated with respect to some partitioning of the feature space -- and expose how the calibration assumptions propagate into the teaming framework. In particular, we consider frameworks that either (i) combine human and model predictions or (ii) delegate prediction responsibility to either a human or model. We show via theoretical and empirical results that existing methods for combination do not preserve the human's degree of calibration. Methods for delegation (by the very act of delegation) preserve calibration of the downstream predictors but shift the burden onto the rejector meta-model that decides who predicts. The rejector must be calibrated finely enough to locate where each member is superior, a demand that grows with the human's expertise and becomes unattainable when the human relies on information the system cannot observe.

</details>


### [170] [Range Penalization: Theoretical Insights with Applications in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10916)
*Yiyuan She,Zhaojun Hu,Yifan Sun*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种新的联合学习正则化框架（范围正则化与极值聚类），在提高模型统计精度的同时，诱导权重结构化以优化通信、量化及计算资源开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决联合学习中因客户端异构性导致的统计精度下降问题，同时希望通过诱导跨客户端的权重规律性，以提升模型在量化、编码和通信/计算资源分配方面的效率。

Method: 1. 引入了“范围正则化”（range regularization）技术；2. 采用“极值聚类”（polar clustering）方法处理个性化特征的权重；3. 开发了针对非范数（seminorm）正则化项的非渐近统计分析证明技术；4. 设计了利用局部强凸性差异的快速优化算法。

Result: 理论分析证明了该方法的统计一致性和模式恢复能力；实验结果验证了该方法在提升模型精度、降低迭代复杂度以及增强资源利用效率方面的显著优势。

Conclusion: 该论文提出的范围正则化方法有效提升了联合学习的统计精度，通过极值聚类（polar clustering）实现了跨客户端的权重规律性，为模型量化、编码和资源调度提供了有力支持，同时通过新的优化策略降低了计算复杂度。

Abstract: This paper introduces range regularization for federated learning with linear systematic components to enhance statistical accuracy and induce cross-client regularity conducive to quantization, coding, and resource efficiency. Our approach identifies features with shared weights across different clients and adaptively clusters the weights of personalized features at extreme values, a process we refer to as polar clustering. Theoretical analysis of the associated estimators poses significant challenges due to the seminorm nature and non-decomposability of the regularizer. We develop new proof techniques for the nonasymptotic analysis of statistical accuracy and faithful pattern recovery. Moreover, a fast optimization algorithm that leverages varying degrees of local strong convexity is proposed to reduce iteration complexity. Experiments support the efficacy and efficiency of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [171] [Generalized Conformal Predictive Systems Under Distributional Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11044)
*Jef Jonkers,Johanna Ziegel*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出了一种处理非交换分布偏移的扩展保形预测系统，通过引入权重加权和不确定性边界，在保留有限样本或渐近校准保证的前提下，实现了对偏移分布的鲁棒预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的保形预测系统（CPS）依赖于数据具有“可交换性”这一前提假设，而在实际应用（如协变量偏移或反馈系统）中，数据往往不满足该假设，导致传统的CPS失效。因此，需要开发一种能够处理分布偏移并保持校准有效性的预测框架。

Method: 1. 利用观测特定的置换权重（permutation weights）来编码输入数据中的分布偏移。
2. 引入权重不确定性边界（weight-uncertainty boxes）来处理权重估计误差，构建鲁棒的保形预测包络（CPS envelopes）。
3. 针对符合度度量（conformity-measure）、保形分箱（conformal binning）和保形保序分布回归（conformal isotonic distributional regression）推导了高效计算方法。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在协变量偏移和生物分子设计任务中表现出色：在面临更强偏移时，预测区间会自动变宽以保持校准；随着样本量增加，预测区间如预期般收敛（变窄）。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一种能够适应分布偏移的保形预测框架，通过加权机制和不确定性鲁棒性处理，实现了在非交换场景下的有效校准，并通过多项实验验证了其对分布偏移的鲁棒性及样本量增加时的收敛性。

Abstract: Conformal predictive systems (CPS) output calibrated bands of CDFs under exchangeability. We extend generalized CPS to non-exchangeable settings by encoding distributional shifts through observation-specific permutation weights. This yields shift-aware predictive systems that remain valid whenever the test point is, conditionally on the unordered sample, a weighted draw from the observed atoms. Since such weights are typically estimated, we introduce weight-uncertainty boxes and construct robust CPS envelopes with finite-sample or asymptotic confidence guarantees. We derive efficient computation for conformity-measure CPS, conformal binning, and conformal isotonic distributional regression. Experiments under covariate shift and feedback-driven biomolecular design show calibrated predictive bands that widen under stronger shifts and tighten as sample size increases.

</details>


### [172] [Itô maps for any-step SDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11156)
*Zhengkai Pan,Peter Potaptchik,Wenxi Yao,Michael S. Albergo,Jakiw Pidstrigach*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出“Itô映射”，弥补了现有生成模型在随机动力学蒸馏上的空白，通过单步预测技术实现了高效、可微的随机过程控制与后验采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的一步生成模型主要针对确定性流映射，未能解决如何对随机动力学进行精确蒸馏的问题。

Method: 引入“Itô映射”（Itô map），这是一种能够通过单次前向传递（single pass）处理中间状态和布朗路径，从而预测未来状态的任意步数随机流映射。利用该方法通过微分访问后验样本，实现了推理时的有效控制。

Result: 实验表明，Itô映射在固定中间状态下能生成多样且条件有效的终点样本，并在合成数据和图像生成基准测试中展现出强大的可控性（steering performance）。

Conclusion: 提出了“Itô映射”这一概念，证明了将任意步数SDE积分构建为一种基础操作，在后验采样和随机控制任务中具有显著的有效性和实用价值。

Abstract: Recent one-step generative models accelerate sampling by learning deterministic flow maps of the underlying dynamics. These methods rely on learning from ordinary differential equations, leaving open how to define an exact distillation procedure for stochastic dynamics. We introduce the Itô map, an any-step stochastic flow map that takes an intermediate state and Brownian path and predicts future states in a single pass. The Itô map formulation yields novel estimators for inference-time control by providing cheap, differentiable access to posterior samples. Empirically, Itô maps produce diverse, conditionally valid endpoint samples from fixed intermediate states and support strong steering performance on synthetic and image-generation benchmarks. These results establish any-step SDE integration as a useful primitive for posterior sampling and stochastic control.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [173] [Uncertainty-Aware Motion Planning for Autonomous Driving in Mixed Traffic Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09958)
*Ming Cheng,Hao Chen,Ziyi Yang,Ziluowen Luo,Senzhang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对混合交通中人类驾驶意图的高不确定性问题，提出了一种名为UAMP的决策框架，通过建模意图不确定性并校准价值函数，提升了自动驾驶的决策安全性与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的强化学习方法通常将人类驾驶员的预测意图视为确定性状态，但人类意图受行为多样性、感知噪声及部分可观测性影响，具有天然的不确定性，这种确定性处理方式会导致自动驾驶车辆做出不安全的决策。

Method: 1. 提出 proximity-aware uncertainty estimator（邻近感知的各种不确定性估计器），用于量化交互条件下的意图不确定性并构建联合意图分布；2. 引入 Uncertainty-Calibrated Value Learning (UCVL)，通过校准偏向的价值函数学习来处理引入不确定性意图预测后的观测偏差。

Result: 在多种混合交通场景下的实验表明，UAMP 在安全性、驾驶舒适性方面优于现有方法，且保证了交通效率。

Conclusion: UAMP通过显式建模人类驾驶意图的不确定性，有效解决了直接将预测意图视为确定性状态带来的决策偏差，显著提升了自动驾驶在混合交通环境下的安全性、舒适性及通行效率。

Abstract: In mixed-traffic environments where autonomous and human-driven vehicles may co-exist, motion planning for autonomous vehicles requires anticipating the future behaviors of surrounding human drivers. Existing reinforcement learning-based methods generally directly incorporate the predicted human intents into the observation to enable a proactive planning. However, human intent is inherently uncertain due to the behavioral diversity, perception noise, and partial observability. Treating predicted intends as deterministic states can result in unsafe decisions for autonomous vehicles. To address this problem, we propose Uncertainty-Aware Motion Planning (UAMP), which incorporates uncertainty in human intent prediction for AV decision-making. Specifically, UAMP first introduces a proximity-aware uncertainty estimator to quantify the interaction-conditioned intent uncertainty and constructs an uncertainty-guided joint intent distribution over surrounding human-driven vehicles. Within this uncertainty set, UAMP further introduces Uncertainty-Calibrated Value Learning (UCVL) to correct value function learning biases arising from directly incorporating uncertain human intent predictions into the observation. Extensive experiments in various mixed-traffic scenarios show that UAMP significantly improves safety and driving comfort, while maintaining traffic efficiency compared with existing approaches. The code is released at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/UAMP-5638.

</details>


### [174] [GHOST: Hierarchical Sub-Goal Policies for Generalizing Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10025)
*Sriram Krishna,Ben Eisner,Haotian Zhan,Ying Yuan,Haoyu Zhen,Chuang Gan,Shubham Tulsiani,David Held*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GHOST 是一种用于机器人视觉操纵的分层学习框架，通过将策略解耦为高层 3D 子目标预测和低层机体动作控制，实现了更强的泛化能力，并能通过人类视频数据高效扩展训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的平铺式（Flat）模仿学习策略在面对训练分布之外的任务时泛化能力较差，且利用人类演示数据时容易受到动作重定向噪声的影响。

Method: 提出一种分层控制框架：(1) 高层策略：基于多视图 RGB-D 观测预测 3D 末端执行器位姿分布作为子目标；(2) 空间接口：将 3D 子目标投影到图像平面并表示为热图，以供策略使用；(3) 低层控制器：执行与具体机体相关的动作。该框架支持仅在机器人数据上训练低层控制器，而在人类视频数据上训练高层策略（无需动作重定向）。

Result: 在多项操纵任务中，GHOST 相比平铺式 Diffusion Policy 表现出更好的性能和鲁棒性，且能够通过少量人类演示实现对新物体和任务变化的快速适应。

Conclusion: GHOST 框架通过分层控制和基于 3D 空间接口的解耦设计，显著提升了机器人视觉运动操纵策略的泛化能力和鲁棒性，并为利用人类演示数据提供了高效路径。

Abstract: We present GHOST, a framework for learning visuomotor manipulation policies that generalize beyond the training distribution. GHOST factorizes control into (i) a high-level policy that predicts the next sub-goal as a distribution over 3D end-effector poses from multi-view RGB-D observations, and (ii) a low-level goal-conditioned controller that executes embodiment-specific actions. To condition image-based policies on 3D goals, we introduce a simple spatial interface that projects predicted goals into the image plane and represents them as end-effector heatmaps. Across a suite of manipulation tasks, this hierarchical factorization consistently improves performance and robustness compared to a flat Diffusion Policy.
  Further, we show that this hierarchical interface also makes it easy to incorporate human demonstrations without relying on (noisy) action retargeting. As sub-goals are largely embodiment-agnostic, we train the high-level policy on human video to specify how learned skills should be applied and composed, while keeping the low-level policy trained purely on robot data. This hierarchy enables adaptation to novel objects and task variations using a small number of human demonstrations.

</details>


### [175] [Robotic Nonprehensile Object Transportation with a Hanging Tray](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10039)
*Adam Heins,Angela P. Schoellig*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种悬挂式托盘系统，旨在通过物理摆动特性简化机器人“服务员问题”的控制难度，仅用3自由度移动底座即可实现高效、平稳的物体运输（减少滑动与晃动）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的“服务员问题”（物体的非抓取运输）通常需要复杂的6自由度机械臂来模拟摆动以减少剪切力。作者意图通过被动动力学（悬挂式托盘）简化控制需求，仅用简单的3自由度移动底座即可获得与复杂机械臂相当，甚至在减少物体滑动和液体晃动方面性能更优的结果。

Method: 利用绳索将托盘悬挂在移动机器人的末端执行器上，使其形成三维摆系统。通过控制移动底座的加速度，利用悬挂系统的物理特性减小惯性力对托盘上物体的影响，辅以视觉伺服技术实现人机交互。

Result: 仿真与真实硬件实验表明，相比固定刚性托盘，悬挂式托盘显著降低了物理载物的滑动和晃动。此外，系统成功集成了视觉识别与伺服功能，实现了自动寻找需服务的客户。

Conclusion: 通过悬挂式托盘方案，仅利用3自由度移动底座即可实现优异的非抓取式物体运输效果，且该方案能显著减少由于移动产生的滑动和晃动。

Abstract: We consider the nonprehensile object transportation task known as the waiter's problem, in which a robot must move an object balanced on a tray from one location to another. In contrast to prior works on the robotic waiter's problem, which make the robot tilt a tray rigidly held by its end effector (EE), we use a tray suspended from the EE by ropes, such that it behaves like a three-dimensional pendulum. Some prior works have actuated the robot so that the EE simulates the behavior of a pendulum, because pendular motion reduces the shear forces acting on the transported objects, minimizing the sliding of rigid objects and sloshing in containers of liquid. In contrast, our use of a real hanging tray allows us to obtain the benefits of pendular motion while only actuating a 3 degree-of-freedom (DOF) mobile base, rather than requiring a full 6-DOF manipulator arm. Our experiments in simulation and on real hardware show that the hanging tray substantially reduces both sliding and sloshing compared to a static, rigidly-grasped tray. Furthermore, we integrate the hanging tray into an interactive robot waiter demonstration, which uses computer vision to identify people with a raised hand and visual servoing to steer toward them and allow them to access the tray.

</details>


### [176] [Efficient-WAM: A 1B-Parameter World-Action Model with Low-Cost Future Imagination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10040)
*Jiajun Li,Tiecheng Guo,Yifan Ye,Rongyu Zhang,Xiaowei Chi,Qianpu Sun,Ying Li,Yunfan Lou,Yan Huang,Zhihe Lu,Meng Guo,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Efficient-WAM通过将未来视觉预测重构为轻量级的动作控制指导信号，舍弃高保真渲染开销，在保持机器人控制性能的同时实现了推理速度的大幅提升（30倍）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的世界-动作模型（WAMs）过度依赖高保真视觉预测，导致推理延迟高，难以满足机器人实时部署的需求。旨在设计一种高效架构以降低推理成本，同时保留未来预测对动作生成的增益。

Method: 1. 采用从大型预训练模型（WAN-2.2-5B）迁移的紧凑型视频专家；2. 使用稀疏化的视频潜在表示（token-sparse）；3. 实施非对称视频-动作去噪策略，减少视频生成抽样步数；4. 将未来视频预测定位为动作生成的紧凑辅助信号，而非追求视觉保真度。

Result: 在RoboTwin 2.0及真实机器人操作任务中，Efficient-WAM在视频预测较粗糙的情况下仍保持了优秀的动作执行性能；模型仅10亿参数，将推理延迟降低至约100ms，实现了比现有WAMs高30倍的加速。

Conclusion: Efficient-WAM证明了具身控制中的未来预测无需追求高保真度；通过降低视频预测的计算约束，可以在保持强劲控制性能的同时实现显著的推理加速，从而推动具身智能的实时部署。

Abstract: World-Action Models (WAMs) have emerged as a promising paradigm for embodied control by coupling future visual prediction with action generation. However, most existing WAMs rely on photorealistic future prediction, which incurs high inference latency and makes real-time robot deployment difficult. This motivates a more efficient WAM design that preserves the control benefits of future visual prediction while reducing its inference cost. We introduce Efficient-WAM, a World-Action Model that reduces the cost of future imagination while preserving its control benefit. Efficient-WAM improves inference efficiency via a compact video expert transferred from WAN-2.2-5B, token-sparse video latents, and asymmetric video-action denoising that allocates fewer sampling steps to video than to actions. Instead of optimizing the future branch for visual fidelity, Efficient-WAM treats future video prediction as a compact guidance signal for action generation. Comprehensive experiments on RoboTwin 2.0 and real-world manipulation tasks show that Efficient-WAM maintains strong action performance despite visibly coarse future predictions. While maintaining competitive control capabilities, our 1B-parameter model can reduce per-chunk latency to around 100 ms during physical deployment, achieving a 30x speedup over existing WAMs.

</details>


### [177] [Flow Control: Steering Vision-Language-Action Models with Simple Real-Time Inputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10180)
*Jonathan C. Kao,Jason Chan,Andy Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为“Flow Control”的方法，允许用户在不重新训练的情况下，通过简单的外部输入实时引导视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的机器人动作，从而实现更高质量、更高成功率的交互式控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型往往难以在复杂或不确定的环境中完美理解用户意图，且缺乏实时人工干预手段，导致任务执行效率和成功率受限。因此，作者旨在寻找一种无需重训练即可实时引导VLA模型动作的方法。

Method: 引入一种实时流控制（Flow Control）机制，利用通用输入设备（如键盘）引导VLA模型的动作采样过程。该方法通过将用户输入转化为对模型预训练专家动作分布（expert action distribution）的约束，确保生成的动作既符合模型自身的逻辑（高保真），又体现用户的实时意图（高对齐）。

Result: 1. 能够准确、响应迅速地跟随用户输入进行实时控制；2. 对次优输入具有鲁棒性；3. 显著提升了机器人的任务成功率和执行速度；4. 将Flow Control产生的轨迹用于微调，可增强原模型自主决策和执行的能力。

Conclusion: Flow Control为VLA模型提供了一种直观、高效的实时交互机制。该方法无需额外的微调即可实现，既能提升用户的操作成功率和任务效率，又能作为高质量数据源进一步改善模型的自主能力。

Abstract: We introduce flow control of vision-language-action (VLA) models, a simple and effective way to steer VLA actions in real-time through generic inputs, such as a keyboard. This method can be used out-of-the-box and does not require retraining or fine-tuning VLAs. It enables relatively crude user inputs to steer a VLA to align with user intent. The VLA transforms these inputs into action samples drawn from the VLA expert action distribution learned during training, so that the generated actions are high quality (conformity to the action expert distribution) and high fidelity (reflecting the user's intent). We demonstrate that flow control has many desirable properties: (1) flow control accurately and responsively steers robot actions with user inputs, (2) it is robust to suboptimal user inputs, (3) it enables users to steer VLAs to achieve significantly higher success rates and faster task completion, and (4) fine-tuning a VLA on flow control trajectories improves the autonomous policy. Together, these results provide a simple and intuitive way for users to help steer VLA actions, increasing task performance.

</details>


### [178] [Exploration of Foundation Model-Based Robots in Patient and Elderly Care](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10208)
*Zhiwen Qiu,Wei Liu,Yuexing Hao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 尽管基础模型提升了护理机器人的交互能力，但目前的系统仍面临可靠性瓶颈及临床应用缺失，未来研究需聚焦于可靠性评估、责任制自主及与护理流程的深度集成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球人口老龄化带来护理需求激增，尽管基础模型为机器人带来了交互灵活性，但目前尚不清楚这些技术能否转化为实际的临床价值，且护理场景对系统的可靠性、流程兼容性和人类监督有严格要求。

Method: 通过回顾性分析（Perspective），从设计特征、用户体验和护理相关结果三个维度对现有的基于基础模型的护理机器人进行了综合评估与总结。

Result: 目前系统主要将基础模型用作语音驱动的社交辅助交互层，但在多模态感知和物理自主性上仍有限制；研究显示其在可用性和用户参与度上有积极表现，但存在幻觉和交互崩溃等可靠性问题；目前证据多集中在认知参与等近端指标，缺乏验证性的硬性临床结果。

Conclusion: 当前基于基础模型的护理机器人虽在交互上有积极表现，但仍需向建立护理特定评估标准、实现可负责任的自主性以及深度融入医疗工作流程的方向转型，以推动其在临床中的有效落地。

Abstract: Demand for older-adult and patient care is growing rapidly as populations age worldwide. Foundation models are increasingly being integrated into robots and interactive agents, with the promise of more flexible communication and personalized assistance. However, care settings require reliable and workflow-compatible systems with accountable human oversight, and it remains unclear whether current embodied systems can translate technical advances into clinical impact. This Perspective synthesizes foundation model-based care robots across three areas: design features, user experience, and evidence for care-related outcomes. Current systems most commonly use foundation models as conversational and reasoning layers within voice-centered socially assistive embodiments, while multimodal grounding and physical autonomy remain limited. Empirical evaluations report positive usability and engagement benefits, but reliability failures persist across the interaction pipeline such as hallucinations and conversational breakdowns. Evidence for care impact remains concentrated in proximal outcomes such as cognitive engagement and participation, with limited evidence for validated clinical or care-related changes. We argue that future research should transition toward care-specific evaluation standards, accountable autonomy, and integration into care workflows to support more responsive and responsible care technologies.

</details>


### [179] [What Demonstration Curation Metrics Do to Your Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10229)
*Aarav Bedi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究指出，衡量演示数据清洗效果的标准应是其训练出的策略表现，而非简单的缺陷检测准确率（AUROC）；研究揭示了现有评估中因未控制“轨迹长度”变量而导致的指标虚高问题，并证明了有效的清洗能极大还原受污染数据集的策略性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究“检测缺陷数据的指标”是否必然能提升“行为克隆策略”的性能，并揭示现有数据清洗方法评估中存在的虚假正相关性。

Method: 在LIBERO抓取-放置基准上引入受控结构缺陷（过早释放物体），评估七种不同的清洗指标，对比其缺陷检测AUROC与下游行为克隆策略的成功率，并分析轨迹长度对评估结果的混淆影响。

Result: 发现指标的缺陷检测能力（AUROC）与下游策略成功率之间存在显著脱节（如最高AUROC指标策略表现最差）；大多数指标利用“轨迹长度”作为标签的平凡代理，导致AUROC虚高；最优清洗方法可使策略成功率从基础的3.3%提升至90.0%，接近Oracle的93.3%。

Conclusion: 演示数据清洗方法的有效性应通过“下游策略性能”而非“缺陷检测指标”来评估；现有的缺陷检测评估往往因未能控制“轨迹长度”这一混淆变量而导致指标虚高。

Abstract: We study whether demonstration-curation metrics that detect defective training episodes also improve the downstream behavior-cloning policy that trains on the curated data. On a contact-rich LIBERO pick-and-place benchmark with a controlled structural defect (early gripper release during the carry phase), we find that the two quantities are sharply decoupled. The metric with the highest defect-detection AUROC (0.804) produces the worst curated policy (13.3% task success), while a metric with a substantially lower AUROC (0.638) produces a policy that nearly matches the oracle trained on ground-truth clean data (90.0% vs. 93.3%). We further show that five of the seven metrics we evaluate exploit episode length as a trivial proxy for the defect label, a confound that inflates reported AUROCs to near-perfect values and disappears once episode length is controlled. Across all conditions, the contaminated baseline succeeds on only 3.3% of rollouts, and the two best curation methods close this to within 3 percentage points of the 93.3% oracle ceiling. Our results argue that curation methods should be evaluated by the policy they produce, not the defects they flag, and that any curation benchmark must control for episode length before reporting detection accuracy. We release the testbed, all metric implementations, and the evaluation pipeline.

</details>


### [180] [YUBI: Yielding Universal Bidigital Interface for Bimanual Dexterous Manipulation at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10244)
*Takehiko Ohkawa,Jumpei Arima,Yuki Noguchi,Masatoshi Tateno,Makoto Sugiura,Takuya Okubo,Kengo Ikeuchi,Yuma Shin,Hiroki Nishizawa,Naoaki Kanazawa,Yuki Wakayama,Daiki Fukunaga,Koshi Makihara,Tomohiro Motoda,Floris Erich,Yukiyasu Domae,Tatsuya Matsushima,Yohishiro Okumatsu,Kei Ota*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了 YUBI——一种符合人体工程学的指尖对齐夹爪，通过直接映射手指运动实现高灵巧数据采集，并构建了超大规模数据集以支持机器人基础模型的训练与跨平台迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的通用操作界面（UMI）等手持式数据采集系统虽具备成本优势，但其“手枪式”设计在执行精细、灵巧的双手动操作任务时存在人体工程学障碍。

Method: 设计了一种基于“让步式（yielding）”手指驱动机制的指尖对齐夹爪，集成 VR 6自由度跟踪系统，实现了高保真的轨迹数据采集；通过该设备采集了 8,434 小时、120 万个任务片段的超大规模数据集。

Result: 相较于 UMI 夹爪，YUBI 在复杂双手动任务的多功能性、灵巧度和操作效率上表现更优；基于 YUBI 数据集训练的策略实现了在多种机器人平台（UR、Franka、ELEY）之间的零样本迁移。

Conclusion: YUBI 是一种高效、可扩展且符合人体工程学的双手动灵巧操作数据采集方案，通过开源硬件、软件和数据集，为机器人基础模型的发展提供了一个可复刻的路径。

Abstract: We introduce Yielding Universal Bidigital Interface (YUBI), a finger-aligned gripper designed to enable intuitive, ergonomic, and scalable data collection for bimanual dexterous manipulation. While handheld data collection systems such as Universal Manipulation Interface (UMI) enable affordable data collection, their bulky pistol-grip designs can pose ergonomic and usability challenges for fine-grained, dexterous manipulation tasks. To address this, YUBI presents a distinct design principle: yielding, finger-driven actuation that directly maps human finger movements to gripper jaw motion. Using the YUBI devices, we set up a data collection system with integrated VR-based 6 DoF tracking of the gripper, ensuring high-fidelity trajectory data acquisition. We curate a UMI-based dataset of unprecedented scale: 8,434 hours across 1.20M episodes and 119 tasks. Experiments show that YUBI offers advantages over the UMI gripper in versatility for complex bimanual tasks, dexterity, and operational efficiency. A single policy trained on the YUBI dataset transfers across multiple bimanual robots (UR, Franka, and ELEY) simply by mounting the gripper on each platform, confirming that the collected data are directly executable as policy supervision. We release the gripper hardware, data-collection software, and dataset as one integrated stack, offering the open community a reproducible path to large-scale data acquisition for advancing robotic foundation models.

</details>
