Experiments in both synthetic and real-world environments across cross-room, cross-episode, and cross-task scenarios show that AllDayNav achieves success rates approaching $100\%$ and consistently surpasses strong map-based, VLM, and RL baselines in path efficiency and robustness, demonstrating implicit, memory-driven reinforcement learning as a scalable alternative to explicit mapping for reliable lifelong navigation.

</details>


### [218] [Resilient Navigation for Autonomous Farm Robots by Leveraging Jerk-Augmented Models with IMU-Only Disturbance Rejection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10971)
*Batu Candan,Mohammed Atallah,Simone Servadio,Saeed Arabi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种结合加筋模型与自适应测量协方差调整的EKF导航算法，有效解决了农业机器人越野环境下的传感器噪声与干扰问题，提升了定位精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主农业机器人在非结构化场地常面临多传感器失效（GNSS/LiDAR/视觉）及高频剧烈振动，导致传统EKF导航精度下降，亟需一种抗干扰能力更强的鲁棒估计方法。

Method: 提出了一种基于加筋扩展卡尔曼滤波（Jerk-augmented EKF）的导航算法，并引入了多重调节因子（MTF）动态调整测量协方差矩阵，以实时应对传感器故障及环境干扰。

Result: 实验证明，在Salin247机器人的实际数据集上，该方法较基准EKF模型显著降低了3D位置的均方根误差（RMSE），并增强了航位推算的可靠性。

Conclusion: 将加筋（jerk-augmented）建模与多重调节因子（MTF）自适应方法相结合，显著提升了自主农业机器人在复杂越野环境下的导航鲁棒性与精度。

Abstract: Precise state estimation for navigation of autonomous agricultural robots is often compromised by sensor outages (GNSS/LiDAR/Visual) and high-frequency vibrations inherent in off-road environments. This paper proposes a robust navigation algorithm based on a jerk-augmented Extended Kalman Filter (EKF) integrated with a Multiple Tuning Factor (MTF) adaptation method. Unlike standard EKF approaches that assume constant measurement noise, our method dynamically adjusts the measurement covariance matrix in real-time, allowing the system to cope with sudden disturbances and sensor outliers. We evaluate the algorithm using real-world data from a Salin247 autonomous robot. Results demonstrate that jerk-augmentation combined with MTF adaptation significantly reduces 3D position Root Mean Square Error (RMSE) compared to baseline EKF models, providing superior dead-reckoning capabilities.

</details>


### [219] [Language-Driven Cost Optimization for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10974)
*Diego Martinez-Baselga,Khaled Mustafa,Javier Alonso-Mora*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究开发了一种利用LLM将自然语言用户指令转化为自动驾驶MPPI控制器参数的框架，实现了驾驶行为的直观、自适应调节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶车辆运动规划的代价函数（Cost Function）调参过程复杂且缺乏灵活性，难以适应多变的交通环境或满足个性化的用户偏好。

Method: 提出了一种语言驱动的自适应成本设计框架：1. 使用LLM解析场景描述与用户指令；2. 将指令转换为MPPI控制器的参数；3. 引入人机交互（Human-in-the-loop）验证阶段进行非技术确认；4. 支持基于反馈的迭代优化。

Result: 仿真测试表明，该框架能有效地将用户意图转化为预期的驾驶行为，且调整过程直观、准确。

Conclusion: 本研究成功构建了一个连接大语言模型与运动规划器的交互框架，通过自然语言实现了自动驾驶行为的动态调整，有效降低了参数调优的门槛，弥合了技术系统与终端用户之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: The driving behavior of autonomous vehicles is typically governed by the cost function of their motion planner, which encodes objectives such as speed tracking, smoothness, lane keeping, and collision avoidance. However, tuning the parameters that shape this cost function is a challenging task that requires technical expertise, limiting the vehicle's ability to adapt to evolving traffic scenarios or end-user preferences. This work presents a language-driven framework for adaptive cost design in autonomous driving. A Large Language Model (LLM) interprets structured scenario descriptions and natural language user queries to generate the parameters applied to a risk-aware Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) controller. The system incorporates a human-in-the-loop validation stage in which the proposed behavioral changes are described in non-technical language and confirmed prior to deployment. Users may additionally provide feedback either before or after deployment, enabling iterative refinement of the vehicle's motion behavior. The framework is evaluated across multiple queries in realistic driving scenarios to assess its effectiveness. Simulation results demonstrate that the method successfully induces behavioral changes that align with the intended requirements in an intuitive manner, thereby bridging the gap between intelligent vehicle control systems and end users.

</details>


### [220] [Multi-UAV Active Sensing with Information Gain-based Planning and Belief Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10986)
*S. Habibi,L. Marques*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于信息增益的主动路径规划（IGbIPP）与多无人机协同概率映射框架，并通过农业精准作业实测验证，证实了环境不确定性驱动路径规划和高效的信念融合策略能显著提升无人机群的作业感知性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机在执行空间感知任务时受到续航、传感不确定性以及空间覆盖与观测精度之间权衡的限制，需要一套有效的主动感知框架以优化多无人机的协同作业效果。

Method: 1. 采用因子图（Factor-graph）建模空间环境依赖关系及概率信念图。2. 提出基于信息增益的启发式路径规划算法（IGbIPP）进行决策。3. 对比了随机游走（Random Walk）和扫描覆盖（Sweep）基准方案。4. 评估了多种概率信念融合规则（Bayesian, log-odds, Dempster-Shafer）及空间相关性权重策略。

Result: 实验结果表明：1. IGbIPP 在降低熵值和映射误差方面明显优于对比基准方法。2. 扩大视场（FOV）可显著提升真实世界的覆盖质量与地图精度。3. 在协同映射中，简单的等权或偏置空间权重表现出比自适应权重更稳健的性能。4. Bayesian、log-odds 和 Dempster-Shafer 融合规则在协同映射中表现最优。

Conclusion: 本研究证明了基于不确定性驱动的路径规划、合理的传感几何设计、空间建模以及概率融合方法在现实世界多无人机主动感知任务中的有效性。简单的空间权重分配方案在实际应用中往往比复杂的自适应权重更具鲁棒性。

Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) are increasingly used for active sensing and information gathering in spatially distributed environments. Their performance, however, is constrained by limited flight time, sensing uncertainty, and the trade-off between spatial coverage and observation accuracy. This paper presents a real-world validation of a multi-UAV active sensing framework for probabilistic binary terrain mapping, with precision agriculture used as the application case. The environment is represented as a probabilistic belief map, where spatial dependencies are modeled through a factor-graph formulation. UAV decision making is guided by Information Gain based Informative Path Planning (IGbIPP), and the approach is compared with Random Walk and Sweep coverage path planning baselines using both synthetic terrains and real UAV-derived agricultural imagery. The study also evaluates spatial correlation weights and several probabilistic belief-fusion rules for multi-UAV information sharing. Results show that IGbIPP reduces entropy and mapping error more effectively than the baselines, while a wider field of view improves real-world coverage and map accuracy. The results further show that simple equal or biased spatial weights can be more robust than adaptive weights, and that Bayesian, log-odds, and Dempster--Shafer fusion achieve the best cooperative mapping performance. These findings highlight the importance of uncertainty-driven planning, sensing geometry, spatial modeling, and probabilistic fusion for real-world UAV-based active sensing.

</details>


### [221] [Diffusion Forcing Planner: History-Annealed Planning with Time-Dependent Guidance for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11019)
*Zehan Zhang,Neng Zhang,Yaoyi Li,Jia Cai,Zhiling Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 为了解决自动驾驶规划器的时间不稳定问题，该论文提出了 DFP 框架，通过引入历史引导的异构联合扩散模型，在保证规划质量的同时显著提升了运动轨迹的连续性和稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于学习的运动规划器在闭环驾驶中常面临时间不一致性，表现为微小扰动会导致轨迹不稳定；现有的历史条件注入方法容易导致模型过度依赖历史模式而忽略环境上下文。

Method: 提出 Diffusion Forcing Planner (DFP)，该方法将轨迹分解为历史、当前和未来段，并对不同段分配独立的噪声水平；采用异构联合扩散处理历史与未来预测；在推理阶段，通过分类器无关引导 (CFG) 和退火历史信息来控制并平滑未来轨迹。

Result: 在 nuPlan 基准测试中，DFP 在保持竞争力表现的同时，在复杂驾驶场景下生成了更连续、稳定且可控的运动规划轨迹。

Conclusion: DFP 框架通过异构联合扩散过程和历史引导的控制机制，解决了学习型规划器在自动驾驶中的时间不一致性问题，实现了稳定且可控的轨迹规划。

Abstract: Learning-based motion planners, despite recent progress, often suffer from temporal inconsistency. Small perturbations across frames can accumulate into unstable trajectories, degrading comfort and safety in closed-loop driving. Several methods attempt to inject history as a static conditioning signal to stabilize outputs, only to induce the planner to copy historical patterns instead of adapting to environment contexts. To address this limitation, we propose Diffusion Forcing Planner (DFP), a diffusion-based planning framework driven by history-guided control. Specifically, DFP decomposes the full trajectory into history, current and future segments, and assign independent noise levels to each segment. The model jointly denoises the historical and the future segments, enforcing a heterogeneous joint diffusion process. At inference, classifier-free guidance (CFG) is applied to steer future sampling using annealed history in a controllable manner. Closed-loop evaluation and comprehensive ablations on nuPlan show that DFP achieves competitive performance while producing continuous, stable, and controllable motion plans in complex driving scenarios.

</details>


### [222] [A Spiking Neural Architecture for Coordinating Arm and Locomotor Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11034)
*Lea Steffen,Kathryn Simone,Graeme Damberger,Travis DeWolf,Hudson Ly,Chris Eliasmith*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于 SNN 的集成控制架构，利用 NEF 和 SPA 在仿生基底神经节的协调下，首次实现了人形机器人在仿真环境下双足行走与手臂控制的协同工作与任务切换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于 SNN 的电机控制研究多局限于双足行走或手臂控制的独立场景，缺乏两者集成的控制方案，限制了类人机器人在复杂任务中的应用。

Method: 研究采用了基于神经工程框架（NEF）和语义指针架构（SPA）的脉冲控制架构，并集成了生物学启发的脉冲基底神经节模型进行动作选择。系统在 Nengo（神经控制）与 Isaac Sim（仿真）的协同模拟环境中进行了验证。

Result: 系统成功实现了目标触及、连续数字书写、路径跟踪行走以及通过基底神经节去抑制机制实现行走与手臂操作之间的任务切换，是首次在全尺寸人形平台上集成这类控制的研究。

Conclusion: 该研究成功证明了基于脉冲神经网络（SNN）的控制架构能够在一个完整人形机器人平台上集成双足行走与手臂控制，并可通过基底神经节模型实现任务间的自主切换，为在低功耗神经形态硬件上部署复杂的机器人控制系统奠定了基础。

Abstract: Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) coupled with neuromorphic hardware offer energy-efficient solutions for humanoid robot control. However, existing SNN-based motor control systems address bipedal locomotion and arm control in isolation, leaving integrated control of both unaddressed. We present a spiking architecture that coordinates force-based arm control and bipedal locomotion in a simulated humanoid, using the Neural Engineering Framework (NEF) and Semantic Pointer Architecture (SPA). High-level action selection between locomotor and arm control is mediated by a biologically grounded spiking basal ganglia model. We validate the system through co-simulation of Nengo, for the neural control, and Isaac Sim, demonstrating successful target reaching, continuous digit drawing, path-following locomotion, and finally, switching between walking and arm control via basal ganglia disinhibition. To our knowledge, this is the first integrated spiking controller to combine bipedal locomotion and arm control on a full-scale humanoid platform. The full spike-based implementation enables future deployment on low-power neuromorphic hardware.

</details>


### [223] [Generation of Diverse and Functional Robot Designs using Superquadrics Parametrisation and Quality-Diversity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11037)
*Leni Le Goff,Simon Smith,Emma Hart*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究引入超二次曲面（SQs）作为机器人形态的紧凑几何表示，通过结合MAP-Elites算法，有效克服了生成式设计中由于多样性缺失导致的早熟收敛问题，显著提升了机器人设计的质量与多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人生成式设计在巨大的搜索空间中容易陷入局部最优，且传统进化算法难以有效维持种群的多样性，从而限制了功能性机器人设计的发现。

Method: 1. 提出基于超二次曲面（Superquadrics, SQs）的机器人身体形态表示方法；2. 将SQs与MAP-Elites算法相结合，在两个模拟环境中进行实验；3. 对比了SQs与组合模式产生网络（CPPNs）在不同算法架构下的性能表现。

Result: 实验结果显示，SQs与MAP-Elites的组合在两个测试环境中均取得了最高的质量多样性得分（QD-score），在形态多样性和机器人功能性方面均优于对比方法。

Conclusion: 超二次曲面（SQs）作为一种紧凑且可解释的几何表示方法，与质量多样性（QD）算法（如MAP-Elites）相结合，是探索机器人形态设计空间的有效方案，能显著提升生成机器人的多样性与功能质量。

Abstract: Generative design of robots requires navigating a vast search-space, encompassing physical configurations and behavioural parameters. Evolutionary Algorithms (EAs) have shown promising results, but often converge prematurely to a small set of sub-optimal designs. Most EAs fail to maintain sufficient diversity in the population that would allow the discovery of distinct functional robots. To counter premature convergence, we introduce a superquadrics-based representation (SQs) for robot bodies. SQs are interpretable, compact and computationally efficient mathematical representations of 3D geometrical shapes that can be tuned to specific design-spaces. To encourage morphological diversity, we combine this representation with a quality-diversity (QD) algorithm (MAP-Elites). We compare SQs and Compositional Pattern Producing Networks representations as generators of morphologies, combining them with standard EAs and MAP-Elites. In two test environments, we find that using SQs to generate morphology in conjunction with the MAP-Elites algorithm reaches the highest QD-score across both environments, maximising diversity of design and functionality of generated robots. The findings highlight the benefits of using a compact and interpretable geometric representation for exploring a complex design-space and suggest that combining SQs with an explicit diversity mechanism increases the quality and number of designs generated.

</details>


### [224] [A Distributed Multi-UGV Exploration Framework With Loop-Aware Planning and Descriptor-Aided Localization in Resource-Limited Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11088)
*Zhiwei Li,Haiou Liu,Xijun Zhao,Ji Li,Yingze Wang,Boyang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于多UGV系统的分布式探索框架，通过改进的LiDAR描述符与不确定性感知环路闭合策略，显著提升了在受限环境下的协同定位精度与探索效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在未知、无GPS且带宽受限的环境中，多UGV系统的定位漂移会导致地图不一致及探索冗余，目前缺乏高效、鲁棒的分布式协同探索方案。

Method: 1. 开发了一种基于深度图像预对齐的轻量级LiDAR全局描述符，实现稳健的跨UGV地点识别；2. 引入不确定性感知环路闭合选择模块，优化全局任务分配与局部路径规划；3. 构建了耦合描述符辅助环路闭合与环路感知分层规划的分布式框架。

Result: 1. 地点识别模块AR@1/AR@1%分别达到89.9%/95.5%；2. 有效降低了绝对轨迹误差与双向通信流量；3. 相比mTSP基准，整体框架的探索时间和行驶距离分别减少了15%和14%。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的全分布式协同探索框架有效解决了多无人地面车辆（UGV）在复杂环境下的定位漂移及通信受限问题，提升了探索效率和地图一致性。

Abstract: Robust and efficient cooperative exploration with multiple unmanned ground vehicles (UGVs) in unknown, GPSdenied, and bandwidth-limited environments without prior maps remains challenging, as localization drift degrades map consistency and induces redundant coverage. This paper presents a fully distributed exploration framework that couples descriptoraided inter-UGV loop closure with loop-aware hierarchical planning while enabling autonomous localization and exploration. We develop a lightweight LiDAR global descriptor with range-image prealignment to enable robust cross-UGV place recognition under large yaw and lateral variations, and use verified loop closures to maintain globally consistent trajectories and a sparse topological representation. We further introduce an uncertainty-aware crossUGV loop-closure selection module that scores candidate loop closures under pose uncertainty and retains high-utility loop closures as planning anchors for global task allocation and local route refinement. Simulations and real-UGV experiments show that the loop-closure module achieves AR@1/AR@1% of 89.9%/95.5%, distributed optimization reduces absolute trajectory error, the system substantially reduces two-way communication volume, and the overall framework reduces exploration time and travel distance by 15% and 14%, respectively, compared with an mTSP baseline.

</details>


### [225] [RoboNaldo: Accurate, Stable and Powerful Humanoid Soccer Shooting via Motion-Guided Curriculum Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11092)
*Yichao Zhong,Yidan Lu,Yuhang Lu,Tianyang Tang,Haoguang Mai,Yixuan Pan,Tianyu Li,Li Chen,Jingbo Wang,Zhongyu Li,Peng Lu,Hongyang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboNaldo 是一个用于人形机器人高冲击力射门的强化学习框架，通过三阶段课程学习解决了传统方法在稳定性与任务完成度之间的平衡难题，实现了高精度与高速度的真实世界射门表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统运动跟踪驱动的强化学习（RL）难以适应变化的足球位置和时间，而纯任务奖励驱动的 RL 因探索空间过大难以从零开始学习有效的踢球动作，导致人形机器人在进行高冲击、高精度的足球射门时存在挑战。

Method: 提出了一种名为“RoboNaldo”的三阶段运动引导课程强化学习（RL）框架：1. 学习全身踢球先验平衡；2. 适应静止球的自由定位射门；3. 通过运动指令和踢球触发接口扩展至动态球射门。训练过程中采用启发式规划器，推理时支持多种高层控制器。

Result: 在仿真中，自由踢球误差降低了 48.6%，射门速度提升了 2.96 倍。在真实世界测试中，G1 获取了 0.73m（静止球）和 0.86m（移动球）的平均射门误差，且踢球速度高达 13.10 m/s（达到职业球员射门速度的 59-71%）。

Conclusion: RoboNaldo 成功证明了通过三阶段运动引导课程学习，人形机器人可以在不牺牲全身稳定性的前提下，实现高冲击力、高准确度的足球射门，并在真实硬件（Unitree G1）上验证了其鲁棒性与接近职业水准的射门速度。

Abstract: Elite humanoid soccer shooting requires whole-body stability, high-impulse whole-body interactions, and accuracy to targets. Motion tracking-driven reinforcement learning (RL) provides stability in whole-body movement coordination, but a fixed reference makes it hard to adapt to varied ball positions and strike timings; in contrast, task reward-driven RL struggles to explore and discover valid kicks from scratch. We therefore introduce RoboNaldo, a three-stage motion-guided curriculum RL framework for high-impulse humanoid interaction. A single human-kick reference is used as a scaffold and progressively shifts optimization towards shooting performance. The curriculum first learns a stable whole-body kicking prior, then adapts the kick to free-kick settings where the ball is stationary at random positions, and finally extends it to moving-ball shooting through a locomotion-command and kick-trigger interface. A high-level heuristic planner controls this interface during training, while alternative high-level controllers can drive the same low-level policy at inference. In simulation, RoboNaldo demonstrates free-kick shot error 48.6% lower and shoot velocity 2.96x than prior work baselines. In real world on a Unitree G1 with onboard perception, RoboNaldo attains 0.73 m and 0.86 m average target shooting error from 3 m away in free-kick and moving-ball cases, accordingly. And the post-contact ball velocity reaches 13.10 m/s, which is 59-71% of reported professional open-play shot speed. Project page: $\href{https://opendrivelab.com/RoboNaldo}{\text{opendrivelab.com/RoboNaldo}}$.

</details>


### [226] [EM-Fall: Embodied mmWave Sensing for Day-and-Night Fall Detection on Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11109)
*Yanshuo Lu,Yuxuan Hu,Shenghai Yuan,Xinyu Zhou,Kuangji Zuo,Bofan Lyu,XiChen Yuan,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种集成毫米波传感的具身移动机器人跌倒检测框架（EM-Fall），通过主动移动以克服盲区与遮挡，在居家环境下实现了鲁棒、持续的跌倒监测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的跌倒检测系统（如穿戴式或固定式传感器）存在用户依从性低、空间覆盖有限、遮挡及光照敏感等局限性，难以满足居家环境下的持续监测需求。

Method: 提出了 EM-Fall 框架，将毫米波（mmWave）传感技术集成到移动人形机器人中，通过主动调整视角实现监测覆盖；设计了以人为中心的感知流水线，结合轻量级时序建模来处理环境干扰（如宠物运动）和多径效应。

Result: 通过八种真实室内环境的实验测试，构建了居家 mmWave 跌倒数据集，实验结果表明该系统在面临多种环境干扰时，仍能保持健壮的跌倒检测性能，且改善了监测的连贯性。

Conclusion: EM-Fall 证明了具身移动传感范式在提升居家环境跌倒检测的连续性和鲁棒性方面具有显著优势，为机器人辅助安全监测提供了切实可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Falls are one of the leading causes of injury and hospitalization among elderly individuals, making reliable fall awareness an essential capability for safety monitoring in residential environments. However, existing fall detection systems often rely on wearable devices or fixed sensing installations, which may suffer from low user compliance, limited spatial coverage, or degraded performance under occlusion and poor lighting conditions. In this work, we propose \textbf{EM-Fall}, an embodied fall detection framework deployed on a mobile humanoid robot. The system integrates millimeter-wave (mmWave) sensing with robotic mobility, allowing the robot to actively adjust its sensing viewpoint and maintain target observability across rooms and under occlusion. To address interference in complex residential environments, including pet motion and multipath artifacts, we design a human-centered perception pipeline combined with lightweight temporal modeling to capture motion evolution before, during, and after fall events. We evaluate the proposed system across eight real indoor environments with four participants and construct an in-home mmWave fall detection dataset. Experimental results show that the embodied mobile sensing paradigm improves monitoring continuity and maintains robust fall detection performance under diverse environmental conditions. The proposed framework provides a practical solution for robot-assisted safety monitoring in home environments.

</details>


### [227] [JOIN: Anchor-Grasp-Conditioned Joining via Opposition, Inference, and Navigation for Bimanual Assistive Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11151)
*Drake Moore,Matt Cheng,Xiang Zhi Tan,Taşkın Padır*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 为解决轮椅辅助机器人单臂能力不足的问题，本文提出了JOIN系统，通过引入一个可随叫随到的移动辅助臂，结合视觉语言模型与几何规划，实现了高效的异构双臂动态协作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单臂移动机器人无法胜任日常生活（ADLs）中常见的双臂任务（如开罐、倒水），而为轮椅加装固定双臂存在功耗、成本及空间占用的实用性障碍。因此，旨在开发一种无需固定占用空间、能按需协作的异构双臂系统。

Method: 提出一种“按需双臂结合（Bimanual Joining）”系统，将任务分解为“规划、驱动、抓取”三阶段。核心技术包括：(1) 利用VLM进行任务级推理；(2) 设计了“轮椅参考对立评分（Wheelchair-referenced opposition score）”评估抓取策略；(3) 引入“任务条件定向可操作性（Task-conditioned directional manipulability）”以优化辅助臂的摆放位置。

Result: 在Kinova Gen3（锚点臂）与Hello Robot Stretch 3（辅助臂）组成的跨平台系统上测试，JOIN在同物体及不同物体协作任务中成功率达到19/20（优于现有方法的14/20），显著降低了操作员的修正需求。

Conclusion: JOIN系统通过提出的异构双臂协作框架，成功解决了移动辅助机器人中增加辅助臂的硬件局限性，证明了利用视觉语言模型（VLM）与几何约束结合，能够高效实现跨平台的双臂协同任务。

Abstract: Assistive mobility and manipulation platforms have received increasing attention as a means of restoring independence to individuals with disabilities. While effective for many basic activities of daily living (ADLs), a significant percentage of everyday tasks such as opening a jar, pouring a liquid, lifting a tray, or basic meal preparation, is fundamentally bimanual and remains out of reach for any single-arm system. Adding a second arm to a wheelchair is impractical, due to the additional power draw, cost, and the loss of space required for transfers and mobility. We instead propose a heterogeneous, on-demand bimanual system, in which a wheelchair-mounted anchor arm is joined when needed by a summoned mobile manipulator that serves as a complement arm. The central technical problem, which we call bimanual joining, is conditional: the anchor has already committed to a grasp, and the complement arm must choose where to stand and what to grasp to complete the task. We formulate bimanual joining as a three-phase decomposition (plan, drive, grasp) and show that a vision-language model (VLM), coupled with standard geometric tools, provides task-level knowledge sufficient to solve a representative class of bimanual ADLs. Our system JOIN, contributes (i) a wheelchair-referenced opposition score, and (ii) task-conditioned directional manipulability. We evaluate JOIN on a Kinova Gen3 anchor and a Hello Robot Stretch~3 complement on representative same-object and different-object tasks. JOIN accomplished more attempts (19/20) than state-of-the-art methods (14/20) and required markedly less correction by the operator.

</details>


### [228] [TacForeSight: Force-Guided Tactile World Model for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11184)
*Yujie Zang,Yuhang Zheng,Xian Nie,Yupeng Zheng,Shuai Tian,Songen Gu,Chen Gao,Zining Wang,Shuicheng Yan,Wenchao Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对接触密集型操作，本文提出了 TacForeSight 框架，通过引入力反馈强化的触觉世界模型，实现了高效的实时触觉预测与鲁棒的闭环控制策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模仿学习方法在处理接触密集型操作时，未能有效建模全局力感知与局部触觉反馈的非对称时空特性，导致在面对动态接触转换或复杂几何形状时控制不稳。

Method: 提出 TacForeSight 框架，核心包含两个模块：1. TacForceWM（力条件触觉世界模型），利用腕部力矩信号对触觉动力学进行潜空间预测；2. 预测性触觉条件策略，通过交叉注意力模型建模触觉演化，并利用触觉引导门控模块融合视觉与触觉特征。

Result: 在五项机器人代表性作业及三种过程扰动场景中，TacForeSight 的性能均优于当前现有的基准模型，特别是在动态接触干扰下展现出更强的稳定性和适应性。

Conclusion: TacForeSight 证明了通过结合力反馈与触觉世界模型进行前瞻性接触推理，能够显著提升机器人接触密集型操作的鲁棒性和应对动态扰动的能力。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation requires robots to continuously perceive and regulate evolving physical interactions under dynamic contact transitions or complex surface geometries. Recent imitation learning methods improve contact-aware control by incorporating tactile or force feedback, but they rarely model the asymmetric spatiotemporal roles of global force and local tactile sensing. To address this, we propose TacForeSight, a lightweight force-conditioned tactile foresight framework for real-time manipulation. The core component is TacForceWM, a tactile world model that predicts short-horizon tactile latent dynamics from dual-finger tactile observations conditioned on high-frequency wrist force and torque signals. Another key component, the Predictive Tactile-Conditioned Policy, leverages the predicted latents as anticipatory contact priors, models the current-to-future tactile evolution via cross-attention, and adaptively fuses visuo-tactile features through a tactile-guided gating module. By forecasting purely within a compact latent space, TacForeSight enables proactive contact reasoning with efficient real-time inference suitable for high-frequency manipulation control. Real-robot experiments on five representative tasks and three in-process perturbation settings show that TacForeSight consistently outperforms existing baselines, particularly under dynamic contact disturbances. All models and datasets will be made publicly available on the project website at https://tacforesight.github.io/ProjectPage.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [229] [Determination Provenance: From Ambiguity to Algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10270)
*Joseph M. Hellerstein*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 针对多重结果系统，本文提出了“确定性溯源”框架，通过代数工具量化元组对解析逻辑的依赖深度，从而实现对不确定系统行为的稳健性评估与成本分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典数据溯源无法处理系统存在多种合法结果（非确定性）的情况，现有的解释仅限于在结果选定后进行，无法解释产生该特定结果的深层驱动机制。

Method: 引入“确定性溯源”（determination provenance），定义“支持”（support）为元组在不同解析结果下的集合，并将其形式化为交换半环。利用“过滤”（filtration）机制度量元组的“查询相关深度”（query-relative depth）。

Result: 证明了正关系代数遵循过滤机制，实现了组合稳健性分析与解决成本的定量诊断。该框架成功应用于事务隔离级别（transactional isolation）和包含否定语义的逻辑程序（Datalog^¬）。

Conclusion: “确定性溯源”提供了一种统一且稳健的框架，通过代数结构处理多重结果系统中的模糊性，能够有效评估确定性及其对应的成本。

Abstract: Many data systems admit multiple admissible outcomes for the same input: concurrent transactions may serialize in one of many orders; a logic program may have multiple stable models. Classical data provenance cannot even pose its question in such settings -- it explains how a result was derived, but only after something has chosen which result to produce. We introduce \emph{determination provenance} to track the commitments that resolve this ambiguity. A tuple's \emph{support} is the set of resolutions under which it holds. Supports form a commutative semiring, and layered commitments induce a \emph{filtration} measuring each tuple's \emph{query-relative depth} -- how many layers of semantic resolution it depends on. Positive relational algebra respects the filtration, enabling compositional robustness analysis and quantitative diagnosis of resolution cost. We instantiate the framework for transactional isolation and for $\mbox{Datalog}^\neg$; in both, classical semantic variants (isolation levels; negation semantics) correspond to different views of a single shared filtration.

</details>


### [230] [Reconstructing OPC UA Address Spaces from Time-Series Databases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10663)
*Lukas Lürzer,Hannes Unger,Stefan Huber*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 针对 OPC UA 遥测数据在时序数据库中语义丢失的问题，本文提出了 opcua-ts 架构，通过引入稳定性索引技术实现语义信息的持久化与实时访问的重构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPC UA 是主流工业协议，但现有的时序数据库在存储 OPC UA 遥测数据时会丢失语义元数据。由于 OPC UA 命名空间索引的动态特性（会话局部性、重启后失效）以及多源合并时的标识符冲突，从数据库中恢复这些语义信息非常困难。

Method: 提出并实现了一种名为 opcua-ts 的架构，将语义元数据（层级、单位、类型定义）与遥测数据共同存储于通用时序数据库中，使用生命周期稳定的连接键进行关联，并具备将数据重构为实时 OPC UA 端点的功能。

Result: 通过 NodeSet2 XML 往返测试验证了架构的准确性。对锅炉模拟器的实验结果表明，该方法在多源部署下是可行的，能够有效重构原始地址空间。

Conclusion: 本研究提出的 opcua-ts 架构通过引入生命周期稳定的连接键（lifecycle-stable join key），成功解决了在时序数据库中存储 OPC UA 语义元数据的问题，并证明了重构完整 OPC UA 地址空间的可行性。

Abstract: OPC UA has become the dominant open protocol in operational technology. Time-series databases routinely archive OPC UA telemetry but discard the semantic metadata (node hierarchy, engineering units, and type definitions) which gives sensor values their meaning. Recovering this information from a time-series database is non-trivial: namespace indices recorded at the source are session-local and unstable across restarts, and naive merging across multiple source servers results in identifier collisions. We present opcua-ts, an implemented architecture that persists this semantic information alongside its telemetry in a general-purpose time-series database under a lifecycle-stable join key, and that reconstructs the source address space as a live OPC UA endpoint. We characterize the conditions under which the reconstruction is sound across multi-source deployments and validate the approach with a NodeSet2 XML round-trip against the source server. Initial results from a boiler-simulator round-trip indicate that the approach is feasible.

</details>


### [231] [Provenance Tracking in AI Compilers through the Lens of Coalgebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10937)
*Zilu Tian,Liying Liu*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 为了解决AI编译器中计算图剧烈重写导致的溯源难题，论文提出了一种基于观测语义的溯源追踪方法（COVAN），通过代数余模型实现轻量且稳健的起源追踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编译器在编译过程中对计算图进行剧烈的归一化、下移和优化，导致难以追踪张量和算子的来源（provenance），现有方法要么具有侵入性，要么在处理非单射重写时无法保证可靠性。

Method: 提出了一种基于观测语义（observational semantics）而非标识符传播的溯源方法；利用代数余（coalgebraic）模型对图转换进行形式化，并通过双模拟（bisimulation）机制处理非单射图重写过程中的溯源问题，并实现了原型系统COVAN。

Result: 开发了原型编译器COVAN，验证了该方法能够以最小的设计开销在复杂的编译流水线中实现稳定的溯源追踪。

Conclusion: 提出了一种基于观测语义的溯源追踪方法，通过代数余模型和双模拟理论，在保留编译器转换灵活性的同时，实现了稳健且轻量级的张量与算子溯源，为AI编译器调试与验证提供了可行方案。

Abstract: AI compilers aggressively rewrite computation graphs through normalization, lowering, and optimization, making it difficult to track the provenance of tensors and operators across compilation. Reliable provenance is essential for attaching platform-specific postprocessing, debugging compiler behavior, and validating transformations, yet existing solutions are either invasive or ad hoc under non-injective graph rewrites.
  We present a lightweight, generative approach to provenance tracking based on observational semantics. Instead of propagating identifiers through compiler passes, we observe graph transformations and reason about provenance in terms of observable computational actions. We formalize this approach using a coalgebraic model and bisimulation, which preserves provenance even when intermediate nodes are eliminated. Furthermore, we implement this approach in a prototype AI compiler COVAN, demonstrating stable provenance across compilation pipelines with minimal engineering overhead.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [232] [Express Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10944)
*Albert Gong,Annabelle Michael Carrell,Raaz Dwivedi,Lester Mackey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Express 是一款新型工具，可将非因果注意力近似转化为高效的因果形式，通过优化内存与计算，显著提升了长序列大模型在预填充和解码阶段的性能表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果注意力近似方法在保证精度和效率之间难以平衡，且在处理长序列任务时面临内存占用、计算延迟等资源瓶颈。

Method: 提出 Express 工具，通过数学转换方法将非因果注意力近似转化为因果近似；同时结合 Thinformer 近似技术，并采用 I/O 感知的 Triton 实现进行高效训练与推理。

Result: 实现了 $\log^{3/2}(n)/s$ 的近似误差保证，仅需 $O(s)$ 内存和 $O(s^2 \log^2(n))$ 压缩开销；实验表明其在长上下文处理中明显优于 FlashAttention 2。

Conclusion: Express 是一种能够将非因果注意力近似转换为因果注意力近似的通用转换工具。通过与 Thinformer 结合，它在长序列建模的多个关键环节（预填充、KV 缓存压缩、解码）中实现了性能提升，并在实际部署中超越了 FlashAttention 2。

Abstract: We introduce a new tool, Express, for converting a non-causal attention approximation into a causal approximation with matching approximation guarantees. When combined with the state-of-the-art Thinformer approximation, Express improves upon the best known causal attention guarantees, delivering $\log^{3/2}(n)/s$ approximation error with only $O(s)$ memory and $O(s^2 \log^2(n))$ compression overhead for a sequence of length $n$. We pair these developments with an efficient I/O-aware Triton implementation, demonstrate substantial speedups over FlashAttention 2, and use Express to overcome four resource bottlenecks in the language modeling pipeline: long-context prefill, KV cache compression, long-form memory-constrained decoding, and long-form compute-constrained decoding.

</details>


### [233] [Mechanistic Analysis of Alignment Algorithms in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09850)
*Aarush Sinha,Ishan Garg,Veeraraju Elluru,Arth Singh,Kushal Garg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过机制可解释性技术分析了六种流行的偏好优化算法，发现它们通过迥异的内部计算变形（如特征增强或衰减）实现对齐，证实了“行为对齐不等于内部机制同构”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的后训练对齐算法大多被视为“黑盒”，缺乏对其如何重塑语言模型内部计算机制的深入理解。

Method: 通过层级线性探测（layer-wise linear probing）、稀疏自动编码器（SAE）及跨编码器（crosscoders）技术，对比分析了 PPO、DPO、SimPO、ORPO、GRPO 和 KTO 六种偏好优化算法在三个模型家族中的内部计算表现。

Result: 偏好信号集中在模型的中前层或中后层；不同优化目标会导致截然不同的表征迁移：KTO 和 GRPO 通过特征共享增强线性可分性；DPO 和 ORPO 则通过几何旋转和特征衰减降低了可分性；PPO 和 SimPO 基本保持了原始几何结构。这种重构过程具有模型架构依赖性。

Conclusion: 对齐并非一种同质化的干预方式；行为层面的对齐并不意味着内部重构的统一。研究强调了进行特征级审计及开发具备“机制感知（mechanism-aware）”能力优化目标的必要性。

Abstract: Post-training alignment algorithms are predominantly evaluated as black boxes, obscuring how they reshape language models' internal computations. We present a systematic mechanistic analysis of six preference-optimization methods: PPO, DPO, SimPO, ORPO, GRPO, and KTO across three open-weight model families. By integrating layer-wise linear probing, Sparse Autoencoders, and crosscoders, we localize preference representations and quantify alignment-induced geometric transformations in latent space. We find that preference signals consistently concentrate in early--mid or mid--late layers, but different objectives induce qualitatively distinct representational shifts. KTO and GRPO enhance linear separability through constructive feature sharing and sparse, high-salience recruitment. In contrast, DPO and ORPO degrade separability via non-constructive geometric rotation and feature attenuation, while PPO and SimPO largely preserve baseline geometry. These transformations exhibit architecture-dependent variability, demonstrating that behavioral alignment does not imply uniform internal restructuring. Our findings establish alignment as a heterogeneous intervention, motivate standardized feature-level auditing for safety and interpretability, and highlight the need for mechanism-aware optimization objectives.

</details>


### [234] [SynIB: Informational Bottleneck for Maximizing Synergy in Multimodal Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09853)
*Konstantinos Kontras,Teodora Gagaleska,Thomas Strypsteen,Christos Chatzichristos,Matthew Blaschko,Maarten De Vos,Paul Pu Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对多模态学习中过度依赖单模态线索的问题，本文提出Synergistic Information Bottleneck (SynIB)，通过一种遮盖模态并惩罚置信度的训练目标，促使模型直接学习关键的跨模态协同信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态模型大多侧重于架构层面的融合设计，但标准训练目标往往偏向于利用模态间的冗余信息，难以捕捉仅在多模态联合使用时才存在的“协同信息”（Synergy）。

Method: 提出Synergistic Information Bottleneck (SynIB) 训练目标。该方法在保留标准任务损失的同时，通过在训练中强制遮盖单个模态，并惩罚模型在丢失模态时的预测置信度，从而抑制对单一模态冗余信息的依赖，迫使模型学习跨模态的协同特征。

Result: 在合成的XOR任务中，SynIB成功提取了协同信息；在Hateful Memes、MultiBench情感分析及CREMA-D嘲讽检测等Benchmark上，SynIB将协同依赖样本的准确率提升了最多7.8%，整体准确率提升了最多3.8%。

Conclusion: SynIB通过直接优化多模态协同信息，有效解决了多模态模型过度依赖单模态线索的问题，在合成任务和五项真实世界任务中均表现优异，证明了该方法在提升模型跨模态推理能力方面的有效性。

Abstract: A central objective in multimodal learning is to capture synergy: task-relevant information that arises only from the joint use of multiple modalities, and is not available from any single modality alone. While most approaches operate at the architectural level through larger or more complex fusion models, we propose a complementary axis: shaping the training objective itself. Standard training often emphasizes unimodal or redundant information, falling short on examples that require cross-modal reasoning. We formalize multimodal synergy through information theory and introduce the Synergistic Information Bottleneck (SynIB), a scalable objective that targets synergy directly. To prioritize learning synergy, SynIB motivates the model to predict accurately from all modalities while penalizing confidence when information from any modality is withheld. Alongside the standard task loss, the model runs forward passes with one modality masked at a time and is penalized for remaining confident, which would indicate reliance on unimodal cues rather than cross-modal interactions. We validate SynIB in two regimes. On synthetic XOR tasks where the ground-truth synergy is known by construction, standard training fails to recover it while SynIB does. On five real-world benchmarks, including three MultiBench affective tasks, Hateful Memes with CLIP-ViT and DeBERTa backbones, and a controllable irony extension of CREMA-D we introduce, SynIB improves accuracy on synergy-dependent examples by up to 7.8% and overall accuracy by up to 3.8%.

</details>


### [235] [Uncertainty-aware Multi-fidelity Closure via Conditional Normalizing Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09857)
*Jice Zeng,Shady E. Ahmed,David Barajas-Solano,Panos Stinis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对降阶模型（ROM）的闭包问题，提出了一种基于条件归一化流的多保真度学习框架，通过残差学习显著提升了预测精度，并实现了对模型预测结果的不确定性量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降阶模型（ROM）常因截断误差和多尺度系统中非解析尺度交互的缺失而导致精度不足，即“闭包问题”。现有的方法难以有效刻画低保真模型与高保真解之间的复杂映射及相关不确定性。

Method: 提出了一种基于条件归一化流（Conditional Normalizing Flow）的多保真度学习框架。开发了两种闭包校正策略：直接学习（从低保真系数直接映射高保真系数）和残差学习（学习两者间的差异），并集成不确定性量化机制。

Result: 在二维Navier-Stokes方程模拟的涡旋合并问题中，所提方法均优于未校正的ROM。残差学习策略比直接学习具有更好的性能。该方法不仅提升了预测准确度，还能提供可靠的不确定性评估。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种基于条件归一化流的不确定性感知多保真度（MF）框架，成功解决了降阶模型（ROM）中的闭包问题。残差学习策略在提高预测精度和量化不确定性方面表现出优越性，为高精度、可信的降阶建模提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Reduced-order models (ROMs) provide an efficient surrogate for complex multiscale systems, but their predictive accuracy is often compromised by truncation errors and the inadequate representation of interactions between resolved and unresolved scales. The missing effect of truncated (unresolved) scales on ROM (resolved) scales is often denoted as the closure problem. In this work, we formulate ROM closure modeling as a multi-fidelity (MF) learning problem and propose an uncertainty-aware MF framework based on conditional normalizing flow to enhance ROM predictive accuracy. The proposed approach learns a probabilistic mapping from low-fidelity (LF) ROM coefficients to high-fidelity (HF) coefficients, thereby improving predictive fidelity while quantifying the uncertainty associated with the learned closure. Two correction strategies are investigated: direct learning, in which HF coefficients are predicted directly from LF inputs, and residual learning, which learns the discrepancy between LF and HF coefficients and uses it to recover the corrected HF solution. The framework is demonstrated on a vortex merging problem governed by the two-dimensional Navier Stokes equations. Results show that both correction strategies improve ROM accuracy over uncorrected ROM, with residual learning achieving consistently better performance than direct learning. Moreover, the two proposed deep generative model-based strategies provide uncertainty quantification for the corrected ROM coefficients, which is critical for assessing prediction confidence and supporting the reliable use of ROMs in practical applications.

</details>


### [236] [Mitigating Manifold Departure: Uncertainty-Aware Subspace Rectification for Trustworthy MLLM Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09859)
*Yingxuan Zhuang,Jingxiao Yang,Miao Pan,Cheng Tan,Yuxiang Cai,Siwei Tan,Chen Zhi,Xuhong Zhang,Jianwei Yin,Jintao Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对 MLLM 的幻觉问题，本文提出 MGAP 解码策略。通过几何感知方法自适应过滤有害语言先验，解决盲目抑制导致的“流形偏离”问题，在无需额外训练的前提下提升了模型输出的忠实度与连贯性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型 (MLLMs) 常出现幻觉，归因于对语言先验的过度依赖。现有的去偏策略（盲目抑制先验）会破坏语义流形（Manifold Departure），导致模型性能下降。论文旨在解决如何区分并仅抑制有害先验，同时保护模型整体语义一致性的问题。

Method: 提出 Manifold-Guided Adaptive Projection (MGAP) 方法：1. 通过奇异值分解 (SVD) 构建语言先验子空间；2. 在解码过程中，将多模态隐状态投影到该子空间；3. 使用一致性感知门控机制 (Consistency-aware Gate) 自适应地衰减有害的先验分量，同时保留正交的语义信息。

Result: 在 POPE 和 CHAIR 基准测试中，MGAP 相比现有的解码基准方法，在有效抑制幻觉的同时保持了更好的生成连贯性。

Conclusion: MGAP 是一种有效的免训练解码策略。它通过几何感知的方式选择性地抑制语言先验的负面影响，在缓解多模态大模型幻觉问题的同时，避免了对模型语义结构的破坏，实现了幻觉抑制与生成质量之间的平衡。

Abstract: MLLMs frequently hallucinate objects inconsistent with visual inputs. This issue is typically attributed to the over-reliance on language priors, which can override the visual context. Recent training-free decoding strategies address this by penalizing language priors. However, these methods overlook the dual nature of language priors, where they can be both helpful and harmful depending on the alignment with visual evidence. In particular, blindly suppressing language priors often disrupts the model's semantic manifold, leading to performance degradation, a phenomenon we term Manifold Departure. To address this, we propose Manifold-Guided Adaptive Projection (MGAP), a geometry-aware, training-free decoding method that mitigates hallucinations while preserving representation structure. MGAP first constructs a language-prior subspace from blind hidden states via SVD. During decoding, MGAP projects each multimodal hidden state onto this subspace and applies a consistency-aware gate to adaptively attenuate only the projected prior component, yielding a subspace-selective update that largely preserves the orthogonal semantic components. Extensive experiments on POPE and CHAIR show that MGAP outperforms prior decoding baselines, achieving stronger hallucination suppression without sacrificing coherence.

</details>


### [237] [Flash-GMM: A Memory-Efficient Kernel for Scalable Soft Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10896)
*Gal Bloch,Ariel Gera,Matan Orbach,Ohad Eytan,Assaf Toledo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Flash-GMM通过Triton内核融合技术消除了GMM计算中的内存瓶颈，大幅提升了大规模数据聚类的速度与容量，并为ANN检索带来了性能与召回率的双重优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GMM实现方式在处理大规模数据时，因需要显式物化巨大的责任矩阵，导致严重的内存瓶颈，限制了模型处理数据集的规模与计算速度。

Method: 开发了一种融合的Triton内核，通过“内存融合”（fused kernel）技术，在单次GPU执行过程中完成计算，直接消除了显存中存储巨大责任矩阵（responsibility matrix）的需求。

Result: 相比现有实现，速度提升20倍；数据处理规模提升超过100倍；在ANN检索任务中，相比k-means，可降低1.7倍距离计算开销或提升+2至+12的召回率（Recall@10）。

Conclusion: Flash-GMM成功通过优化内存访问模式，极大提升了GMM的训练效率与扩展性，证明了软聚类（GMM）在ANN检索任务中可作为高效替代k-means的方案，且能通过多聚类分配显著提升检索准确率。

Abstract: We present \textbf{Flash-GMM}, a fused Triton kernel for efficient computation of Gaussian Mixture Models (GMMs) over large-scale data in a single GPU pass. By eliminating the need to materialize the full responsibility matrix in GPU memory, Flash-GMM achieves a \textbf{20$\times$} speedup over existing implementations and enables training on datasets more than \textbf{100$\times$} larger than previously feasible on one device. To demonstrate its impact, we integrate Flash-GMM into the IVF coarse quantizer for approximate nearest-neighbor (ANN) search. We show that soft GMM clustering is now a viable drop-in replacement for $k$-means, and that GMM responsibilities can be leveraged to assign border vectors to multiple clusters. Our approach reaches fixed recall targets with up to $1.7\times$ fewer distance computations, or equivalently, yields $+2$--$12$ recall@10 at matched computational cost. We release the kernel as an open-source project.

</details>


### [238] [Conformal Risk Prediction for Non-Alcoholic Fatty Liver Disease Using Gradient Boosting with Distribution-Free Coverages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09860)
*Xinze Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究开发了一种融合 GBDT 与共形预测的 NAFLD 风险评估框架，在实现高预测精度的同时提供了统计学上的置信保障和临床可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球 NAFLD 发病率高（约25%），但目前缺乏有效的群体级筛查工具。当前模型通常缺乏解释性和统计上的不确定性量化。

Method: 提出了一个结合梯度提升决策树（GBDT）与共形预测（Conformal Prediction）的机器学习框架。该方法采用基于互信息的稳定性选择程序（通过自助重采样）筛选关键临床特征，确保预测结果具备分布无关的校准覆盖率。

Result: 在广州多中心队列中，Method 的 AUC 达到 0.912（内部）和 0.891（外部），表现优于深度神经网络、TabNet、SVM 和逻辑回归。在 90% 的名义水平下，经验覆盖率达到 91.3%。三级风险分层显示高风险组的 12 个月进展率是低风险组的 4.7 倍。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的 Method 框架不仅在非酒精性脂肪肝（NAFLD）风险预测方面优于现有模型，还通过共形预测提供了可靠的置信区间，为临床筛查和个性化风险分层提供了一种高精度、可解释且具有统计保证的工具。

Abstract: Non-alcoholic fatty liver disease (NAFLD) affects roughly 25% of global adults, posing substantial hepatic and cardiovascular risks. Yet, population-level screening tools remain inadequate. We present Method, a machine-learning framework for NAFLD risk prediction coupling gradient-boosted decision trees with conformal prediction to yield calibrated, distribution-free coverage guarantees on individual risk estimates. It integrates a mutual-information-based stability selection procedure to identify a compact, clinically interpretable feature subset via bootstrap resampling, constructing prediction sets whose marginal coverage provably exceeds a user-specified confidence level. We evaluated Method on a multicenter cohort from Guangzhou, China (primary n=2,187; external validation n=412) using 78 candidate features across demographics, metabolic biomarkers, and lifestyle factors. Method achieves an AUROC of 0.912 internally and 0.891 externally, outperforming deep neural networks, TabNet, support vector machines, and logistic regression. Conformal prediction sets achieve 91.3% empirical coverage at the 90% nominal level. A three-tier risk stratification derived from these scores separates the population into distinct groups, with the high-risk subgroup showing a 12-month progression rate 4.7 times that of the low-risk tier. The selected features -- notably waist circumference, ALT, GGT, triglycerides, fasting glucose, and BMI -- align with established metabolic risk factors, providing biological plausibility.

</details>


### [239] [Time Series as Language: A Universal Tokenizer for General-Purpose Time Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09861)
*Yunhao Zhang,Ruiying Qi,Jiale Zheng,Jianfeng Zhang,Lujia Pan,Junchi Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了UniTok，一种通用的时间序列离散化分词器，并配合UniTok-FM基础模型，通过下一词预测（NTP）实现了在时间序列任务上无需微调的零样本及少样本预测、生成与分类能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的时间序列模型通常针对特定任务设计，缺乏像大语言模型通过下一词预测（NTP）实现的通用泛化能力，且难以直接应用于连续、无界的时间序列。

Method: 1. UniTok（通用分词器）：设计了基于向量量化自编码器（VQ-VAE）的架构，引入前缀归一化（scale stabilization）、渐进式分辨率因果编码/解码以及结构保持重构损失；2. UniTok-FM（基础模型）：直接使用常规大语言模型架构，在由具有相似模式的时间序列组成的上下文窗口上进行下一词预测训练。

Result: UniTok-FM在预测、生成和分类任务中表现优异，不仅超越了统计学模型和有监督基准模型，与特定任务的基础模型表现持平，且独具无需微调的上下文内推理能力。

Conclusion: UniTok-FM实现了时间序列分析的通用性，证明了通过将时间序列离散化并利用原生大语言模型架构进行下一词预测（NTP），可以实现跨任务的零样本/少样本推理，无需针对特定任务进行微调。

Abstract: While Next-Token Prediction (NTP) has unified LLM pretraining, its adaptation to unbounded, continuous time series (TS) remains open. To bridge the gap, we introduce UniTok, a universal tokenizer that transforms TS into discrete tokens, and UniTok-FM, a foundation model pretrained via NTP on these tokens. UniTok-FM is a general-purpose foundation model that supports zero-shot and prompt-boosted forecasting, as well as few-shot generation and classification via training-free in-context inference--a capability not achieved by prior works. Technically, UniTok is a vector-quantized autoencoder incorporating prefix normalization for scale stabilization, a progressive-resolution causal architecture for encoding and decoding, and a structure-preserving reconstruction loss for training. UniTok-FM adopts an off-the-shelf LLM architecture without TS-specific modifications. Instead of pretraining on isolated TS, it performs NTP on context windows formed by multiple series with similar patterns, aiming to capture their shared dynamics. Experiments on forecasting, generation, and classification show that a single unified UniTok-FM consistently outperforms statistical and supervised baselines, achieves competitive performance with task-specific foundation models, and uniquely enables training-free in-context inference across tasks.

</details>


### [240] [Blurry Window Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09862)
*Axel Laborieux,Christos Sourmpis,Juan Gabriel Kostelec,Qinghai Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对 Transformer 在长序列中的二次计算瓶颈，本文提出 BLA 机制，利用狄利克雷核实现了一种高效、具备信息回溯能力的状态压缩方案，显著优于传统的滑动窗口方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer 的 Softmax 注意力存在序列长度的二次复杂度及 KV Cache 随序列增长导致的状态爆炸问题；现有的线性复杂度模型（如 SSM/LA/ABC）虽然能达到类似的困惑度，但在长序列信息检索和回溯任务上表现欠佳。

Method: 提出 BLA（模糊窗口注意力），其灵感源于状态空间模型（SSM）。该方法利用狄利克雷核（Dirichlet kernels）对频率窗口进行插值，从而重建“模糊”的 KV 历史记录。BLA 可被视为一种 SWA 的广义化，也可视为门控槽注意力（GSA）的特殊实现。

Result: 在 MQAR 任务上，BLA 的状态效率比 SWA 高 8 倍，且与主流线性注意力模型持平。在 RegBench 任务中，BLA 和 SWA 是仅有的表现随状态空间增加而提升的模型。

Conclusion: BLA 是一种高效的注意力机制，成功在有限状态空间下平衡了长文本处理性能与信息检索能力，在人工合成任务（MQAR, RegBench）中展现出优于现有线性注意力模型及滑动窗口注意力（SWA）的缩放特性。

Abstract: The Softmax Attention operation in Transformer language models has a quadratic complexity in the sequence length and a growing state size in the form of KV cache, which becomes a bottleneck in long context scenarios. To overcome this limitation, alternative architectures with linear complexity and finite state size have been introduced, such as State-Space Models (SSMs), Linear Attention (LA), and Attention with Bounded-memory Control (ABC). Though linear models achieve similar language perplexity as Transformers, they are still behind in tasks which require retrieval or recall of specific information. In this work, we introduce Blurry Window Attention (BLA) a novel ABC method inspired by SSMs. BLA stores a frequency window from which a blurry KV history is reconstructed via interpolation using Dirichlet kernels. BLA can be understood as a generalization of Sliding Window Attention (SWA) depending on the Dirichlet kernels resolution or as a special case of the Gated Slot Attention (GSA), where the decay factor is implemented with Dirichlet kernels. We describe in details the theory and efficient implementation of BLA. On the Multi-Query Associate Recall (MQAR) synthetic task, we show that the state efficiency of BLA is 8$\times$ better than SWA and is competitive with popular linear attention models, and in the RegBench synthetic task, only BLA and SWA improve their performance as the state size grows among the linear models we tested.

</details>


### [241] [From Confident Closing to Silent Failure: Characterizing False Success in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09863)
*Laksh Advani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过分析 LLM 智能体的轨迹，研究发现“虚假成功”现象普遍存在且 LLM Judge 难以准确检测；相比之下，基于 TF-IDF 的轻量级检测器在检测精度和效率上均优于 LLM Judge，是更适合生产环境的监控方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体存在“静默失败”问题，即在任务实际未完成时错误地声称完成（虚假成功）。研究者旨在探究这一现象的普遍性，并评估现有评估手段（特别是 LLM Judge）的有效性。

Method: 研究人员分析了两个基准测试（tau2-bench 和 AppWorld）的数千条轨迹，量化了 LLM 代理的“虚假成功”频率；对比了多种 LLM 作为评估者（Judge）的性能，并开发了基于文本频率（TF-IDF）的轻量级检测器进行性能对比。

Result: “虚假成功”现象在不同任务中广泛存在（部分高至 75.8%）；LLM Judge 的检测性能极低（AUROC 最高仅 0.65），且过度依赖表面代理特征；TF-IDF 检测器在任务中取得了 0.83-0.95 的高 AUROC，且延迟比 LLM Judge 低 3300 倍。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的决策者在检测“虚假成功”方面表现欠佳，因为它们倾向于依赖表面特征（如语言信心或动作数量）。相比之下，轻量级的领域特定检测器（如TF-IDF）在检测效率和准确性上明显优于LLM judge。结论建议生产环境应优先采用轻量级检测器作为监控 triage 信号。

Abstract: LLM agents can fail silently by asserting task completion when the environment state shows otherwise. We study this failure mode, false success, across two agent benchmarks: 9,876 tau2-bench trajectories from 8 model families and 1,879 AppWorld trajectories from 4 model families with text-independent ground truth. False success is common but varies by setting: 45--48% of failures in single-control tau2-bench domains, 3% in dual-control telecom, and 75.8% among AppWorld self-assessing coding-agent trajectories with explicit status claims. LLM judges fail reliably: no configuration across 5 judges, 5 prompt strategies, and full task specifications exceeds AUROC 0.65 on tau2-bench, and the same judges reach only 0.54 AUROC on AppWorld API-call traces. Judges rely on surface completion proxies -- confident closing language in tau2-bench and coarse action-sequence volume in AppWorld -- rather than verified state changes. Lightweight TF-IDF detectors achieve task-disjoint AUROC 0.83 on tau2-bench and 0.95 on AppWorld, recovering 4--8x more false successes than the best judge at the same flag rate with 3,300x lower latency. These results suggest that production monitoring should use lightweight, domain-calibrated detectors as triage signals rather than relying on LLM judges as the primary monitor for false success.

</details>


### [242] [Alignment Collapse Under KV Cache Quantization: Diagnosis and Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09864)
*Bruce Changlong Xu,Adarsh Kumarappan,Mu Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现KV缓存量化会以模型特定的方式无声地破坏LLM的安全对齐；为此提出PCR诊断法，通过识别潜在的安全失效机制，在几乎无额外开销的情况下修复了量化带来的安全性降级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的KV缓存量化研究仅关注性能（困惑度/准确率）而忽略了安全性。研究发现低比特量化会掩盖安全对齐的丧失，且缺乏通用的安全位宽标准，现有的性能指标无法预见这种安全失效。

Method: 提出了一种名为“逐通道归约”（Per-Channel Reduction, PCR）的诊断方法，通过20个校准提示词对模型进行三个层面（异常值干扰、异常值重叠、多层稀释）的机制失效模式分类，并基于此调整量化策略以保护安全特征。

Result: 在11个指令微调模型和5个基准测试上证实，低比特量化会导致安全对齐显著下降（如Mistral-7B拒绝率下降15.2%）。PCR方法成功分类了模型失效模式，在所有测试模型中均能引导正确的缓解方向，并在vLLM生产环境中实现了最高97%的安全恢复效果。

Conclusion: KV缓存量化存在安全风险，低比特量化可能导致模型对齐失效，且该风险无法通过传统的困惑度（Perplexity）指标评估。本文提出的PCR诊断方法能有效识别模型特定的失效模式，并指导量化策略的优化，在极低的时间成本下显著恢复模型的安全性。

Abstract: Key-value (KV) cache quantization is widely used to reduce Large Language Model (LLM) inference memory, yet existing evaluations solely focus on measuring perplexity and accuracy without assessing the safety impact. In this study, we explore alignment preservation under KV cache quantization. Across eleven instruction-tuned models (3.8B-72B) and five benchmarks (1,894 prompts), we find that low-bit quantization can silently destroy safety alignment: Mistral-7B loses 15.2% of its refusals at only 1.03x perplexity, and no universal safe bit-width exists, with sharp model-specific phase transitions invisible to standard metrics. We identify that the root cause is geometric: safety features occupy a low-dimensional activation subspace 10^2-10^3x more vulnerable to quantization noise than the full representation space perplexity averages over. Inspired by this observation, we propose Per-Channel Reduction (PCR), a diagnostic that classifies each model into one of three mechanistic failure modes: outlier-crushes-safety, where safety lives in non-outlier channels collaterally damaged by outlier-driven scale factors; outlier-as-safety, where safety overlaps outlier channels and finer granularity cannot rescue it; and multi-layer dilution, where safety is distributed across many layers and per-layer fixes fail. PCR predicts the correct mitigation direction on all nine primary models and one held-out model from an independent family using 20 calibration prompts. PCR generalizes across unseen prompts, models, and production quantizers, including KIVI with up to 97.2% recovery, succeeding where attention-based allocation methods fail. The resulting training-free protocol, requiring approximately 35 GPU-minutes, recovers up to 97% of lost alignment at minimal memory overhead, addressing vulnerabilities confirmed in production vLLM serving with FP8 KV cache on NVIDIA GPUs.

</details>


### [243] [LLM-as-a-Discriminator: When Synthetic Tables Still Look Real](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09865)
*Manel Slokom,Malek Slokom,Thierno Kante*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用大语言模型（LLM）作为鉴别器，通过区分真实数据与合成数据来评估表格数据的隐私风险，并证明了该方法在合理配置下的审计有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成数据虽被用于降低隐私风险，但如何有效审计这些合成表格数据的隐私泄露风险仍是一个难题。目前缺乏能够有效识别合成数据质量及潜在隐私风险的自动化判定手段。

Method: 利用大语言模型（LLM，如LLaMA和Gemini）进行二分类任务，区分表格数据样本是真实（REAL）还是合成（SYNTHETIC）。研究设定了两种场景：C1（仅提供表格数据）和C2（表格数据+分布元数据）。实验对比了CTGAN、TVAE和Gaussian Copula三种生成模型，并与C2ST、记录链接基准及人类审计员进行了对比验证。

Result: 实验显示LLM鉴别效果具有显著的模型差异。Gemini在识别CTGAN和TVAE生成的合成数据时表现出极高（100%）的检测率，体现了其作为审计工具的潜力；而LLaMA的表现则受限于场景设定（C1 vs C2）和数据集特征。LLM方法表现出作为实用隐私审计信号的潜力。

Conclusion: 大语言模型（LLM）鉴别法是一种有效的表格数据隐私审计工具，但其效果受模型选择、元数据呈现方式及数据编码的影响。该方法比传统基准（如C2ST）在特定场景下更能发现合成数据的泄露风险。

Abstract: Privacy and data sharing are often in tension. Many organizations use synthetic data to reduce privacy risk and still share useful data. For tabular data, auditing privacy remains hard. In many cases, even humans cannot easily tell if a table is real or synthetic. In this paper, we propose a method based on LLM discrimination. We ask an LLM to classify each table sample as REAL or SYNTHETIC. We test two settings: C1 with table only, and C2 with table plus distributional metadata. We use LLaMA as an open model and Gemini as a reference model. In our experiments, we run three synthesis models, CTGAN, TVAE, and Gaussian Copula, on two public datasets, UCI Adult and ACS Census. We collect 451 valid trials. Our results show clear differences between models. On Adult, LLaMA reaches DRS=0% in reported cells, while Gemini reaches DRS=100% for CTGAN and TVAE. On Census, LLaMA predicts SYNTHETIC for most samples, while Gemini stays high in C1 but drops for CTGAN and TVAE in C2. We also compare with a classifier two-sample test (C2ST) and record linkage as distributional baselines, and with a human pilot of 2 annotators and 240 trials. Our results show that LLM discrimination is a practical privacy audit signal when model choice, per provider reporting, and data encoding are handled with care. For reproducibility, code and experiment scripts are available at https://github.com/SlokomManel/LLM-as-a-Discriminator.

</details>


### [244] [Two to Tango: Coupled Task-Reference Selection for Safe LLM Fine-tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09866)
*Xinrui Chen,Jianhao Zhang,Ou Wu,Di Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对大模型微调导致的安全对齐失效问题，本文提出了 DualSelect 框架，通过联合选取任务样本与动态安全参考样本，在提升任务性能的同时高效保持了模型的安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大模型微调阶段，为了提升下游任务的表现，往往会导致模型原有的安全对齐（Safety Alignment）能力下降。现有方法如简单的全局约束或单一过滤存在局限性，无法动态应对不同任务带来的不同安全约束挑战。

Method: 提出 DualSelect 框架，采用联合选择机制：基于极大极小（minimax）视角，通过熵正则化评分代理、延迟参考更新（lazy reference refresh）和梯度校正来筛选高损失安全参考样本和兼容的任务样本，确保更新方向符合安全约束。

Result: 在 1B-8B 参数规模的模型上，DualSelect 在保持任务效用（Utility）的同时取得了卓越的安全性能。使用 REDORCA 评测结果显示，其安全性平均分比最强基线提升了至少 5.10 分，并在多种评测指标下保持领先。

Conclusion: DualSelect 通过联合选择任务样本和安全参考样本，有效解决了微调过程中大模型安全性能衰退（Safety Alignment Erosion）的问题，并在保持任务性能的同时显著提升了模型的整体安全性。此研究也为持续学习中的知识保持提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Fine-tuning safety aligned large language models (LLMs) on downstream data improves adaptation but may erode learned safety behavior. Existing methods use fixed safety examples, global constraints, or one-sided task filtering. Our diagnostics show task updates expose different safety constraints, motivating joint selection of relevant references and compatible task samples. We propose DualSelect, a coupled framework for task and reference selection that refreshes task conditioned safety references before filtering whole task samples compatible with the induced reference direction. Under a minimax view, DualSelect selects safety references with high preservation loss and task conflict, together with compatible task samples, through entropy-regularized scoring surrogates, lazy reference refresh, and gradient correction. On 1B-8B LLMs, DualSelect preserves safety without losing task utility; using the REDORCA judge, it improves Safety Avg. over the strongest baseline by at least 5.10 points and remains highest in Safety Avg. across judges with moderate overhead. This view extends to retention focused continual learning.

</details>


### [245] [Disjoint or Overlapping? Inference Windowing for Reconstruction-Based Time Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09874)
*Guillaume Coulaud,Reza Akbarinia,Florent Masseglia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了单变量时间序列异常检测中的重构类方法，发现“推理步长”是影响性能的关键因素。通过建立统一的评估协议，作者证明重叠滑动窗口可显著提升重构模型的检测表现，并强调了标准化评估流程对于该领域研究的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前重构型异常检测方法的评估实践不统一，且推理程序（如推理步长的选择）描述模糊，导致不同研究间的实验结果难以比较，且未充分探讨推理策略对检测性能的影响。

Method: 1. 在TSB-AD基准测试上建立统一的训练、调参和多随机种子评估协议；2. 对比分析多种模型（PCA、DLinear、AutoEncoder、TimesNet、Transformer等）在不同推理步长（重叠与不重叠窗口）下的表现；3. 在全量UCR存档上利用与滑动窗口重构一致的定位标准进行扩展评估。

Result: 1. 推理过程使用重叠窗口（overlapping windows）可使所有测试模型的平均相对性能提升高达28%，甚至改变方法间的排名；2. 揭示了跨数据集、随机种子和超参数配置的性能变异性；3. 证明了重构型基准方法在经过规范化评估后，在单变量时间序列异常检测中具有强劲的竞争力。

Conclusion: 重构型异常检测的性能不仅取决于模型架构与训练，推理策略（如推理步长）同样至关重要。研究证明重构方法在单变量时间序列异常检测中极具竞争力，通过规范化的评估协议，重构模型可以在TSB-AD和UCR基准测试中获得稳健性能。

Abstract: Reconstruction-based methods are widely used for time series anomaly detection, where models are trained to reconstruct subsequences, and anomalies are identified through reconstruction errors. However, reported results are often hard to compare due to heterogeneous evaluation practices and underspecified inference procedures. In this paper, we revisit reconstruction-based anomaly detection in the univariate offline setting and study the role of the inference stride, which controls whether subsequences are processed as disjoint windows or with overlap. We propose a unified training, tuning, and multi-seed evaluation protocol on the curated TSB-AD benchmark, and study how overlapping inference affects anomaly detection performance for a range of reconstruction models, including PCA-based baselines, DLinear, an AutoEncoder, TimesNet, and Transformer variants. The results show that across all models, overlapping windows yield consistent improvements, with average relative gain up to +28%, and can alter method rankings. We further analyze variability across datasets, random seeds, and hyperparameter configurations. Finally, we complement the benchmark study with an evaluation on the full UCR archive using localization criteria aligned with sliding-window reconstruction. Overall, our results highlight that reconstruction-based anomaly detection performance depends not only on model architecture and training, but also on inference choices, motivating a clear and reproducible protocol. Our results show that reconstructionbased baselines achieve strong performance on both TSB-AD and UCR benchmarks, supporting them as competitive and practical approaches for univariate time series anomaly detection.

</details>


### [246] [SPACE: Source-free Proxy Anchor Concept Erasure for MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09868)
*Zhijing Zhang,Jiaqi Ding,Qianshan Wei,Nan Zhou,Jiaqi Li,Yongliang Wu,Tongxin Zhu,Xiaolin Fang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对MLLM无法获取敏感数据进行机器学习遗忘的问题，本文提出了SPACE框架。该方法通过选择代理锚点并在保留知识的零空间内进行语义隔离优化，在无源情况下实现了高效且安全的概念擦除，同时保持了模型整体性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM在应用中面临日益严峻的隐私风险与法规限制。现有的机器遗忘（MU）方法普遍依赖目标概念的视觉数据，但由于严格的数据保留限制，往往无法获取这些数据，因此亟需一种无需访问原始目标数据的无源机器遗忘方法。

Method: 提出了SPACE框架，包含两个核心阶段：(1)文本引导代理锚点选择（TPAS）：在共享特征空间中检索语义对齐的代理锚点以替代原始数据；(2)双重约束语义隔离（DCSI）：将模型更新约束在保留知识的零空间（null space）内，同时优化代理锚点以实现目标概念的间接擦除。

Result: 在六个数据集上的广泛实验表明，SPACE在无源场景下达到了与最先进的数据依赖型遗忘方法相当的性能。

Conclusion: SPACE是首个针对MLLM的无源（Source-free）机器遗忘框架。它通过理论证明其能严格限制对保留知识的干扰，并最大化特征谱熵，从而在无需原始目标数据的情况下实现了与现有数据依赖型技术相当的遗忘效果，为解决数据隐私与合规性问题提供了有效的技术手段。

Abstract: As Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) face growing privacy risks and regulatory constraints, machine unlearning (MU) has emerged as a crucial solution for removing sensitive data while preserving model performance. However, existing MU methods typically rely on visual data of the target concepts, which is often unavailable due to strict data retention policies, thus creating a demand for source-free unlearning approaches that operate without access to the target data. In this work, we propose Source-free Proxy Anchor Concept Erasure (SPACE), the first source-free unlearning framework specialized for MLLMs. SPACE consists of two stages: (1) Text-Guided Proxy Anchor Selection (TPAS), which retrieves semantically aligned proxy anchors from the shared feature space. (2) Dual-Constraint Semantic Isolation (DCSI), which optimizes these anchors to indirectly erase target concepts. DCSI confines updates to the null space of retained knowledge, ensuring structural integrity. We theoretically prove that SPACE strictly bounds the perturbation on retained knowledge and maximizes feature spectral entropy, thereby maintaining the model's performance. Furthermore, extensive experiments across six datasets show that SPACE achieves performance comparable to that of state-of-the-art data-dependent methods, validating its effectiveness in source-free MU scenarios. The source code will be released.

</details>


### [247] [Inverse Probability Weighting and Age-of-Information Aggregation for Decentralized Federated Learning under Partial Reception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10774)
*Chanuka A. S. Hewa Kaluannakkage,Rajkumar Buyya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对有损无线网络下 DFL 的选择偏差与模型陈旧挑战，本文提出了 DFL-AA 算法，通过 IPW 与 AoI 加权技术实现高效、去中心化的联合学习优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的分散式联邦学习（DFL）在有损无线网络中存在严重的“选择偏差”（由于链路质量差导致模型部分接收）和“模型陈旧”（异步节点贡献过期信息）问题，导致模型性能下降。

Method: 提出 DFL-AA 算法，结合了逆概率加权（IPW）和基于 EWMA 的在线信道估计来校正选择偏差，并引入基于信息年龄（AoI）的加权机制来处理模型陈旧。

Result: 理论上证明了 DFL-AA 在期望上消除了链路质量畸变；实验结果显示，该算法在不同的丢失率、网络规模和异构无线条件下均表现出优于现有最先进算法（SOTA）的性能。

Conclusion: DFL-AA 有效消除了链路质量导致的偏差，并通过基于 AoI 的加权机制减轻了模型陈旧问题，在多种网络环境下优于现有的基准算法。

Abstract: Decentralized Federated Learning (DFL) over lossy wireless networks faces two key challenges: selection bias, where updates from poor-quality links are systematically underrepresented due to partial model reception, and update staleness, where asynchronous nodes contribute outdated information. We show that uniform gossip aggregation with local-fill reconstruction introduces persistent link-quality-induced bias, while completeness-based weighting further amplifies this effect. To address these challenges, we propose DFL-AA (Decentralized Federated Learning with Adaptive AoI-weighted Aggregation), which combines Inverse Probability Weighting with online EWMA-based channel estimation to correct selection bias and Age-of-Information-based weighting to mitigate staleness without requiring global synchronization. We theoretically show that DFL-AA removes link-quality distortion in expectation and experimentally demonstrate consistent improvements over state-of-the-art baselines across varying loss rates, network sizes, and heterogeneous wireless conditions.

</details>


### [248] [Integrating Local and Global Entropy for Uncertainty Quantification in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09875)
*Johanne Medina,Tianyi Zhou,Keivin Isufaj,Aristides Gionis,Sanjay Chawla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了GLU（全局-局部不确定性）方法，通过结合模型隐状态的几何复杂度和token级别的熵，提升了大型语言模型幻觉检测的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的不确定性量化（UQ）方法过于依赖 token 级信号，忽略了隐层状态的几何结构信息，导致难以检测“自信但错误”的幻觉模式。

Method: 提出Global-Local Uncertainty (GLU)方法，该方法通过乘法门控机制融合 token 级熵（局部不确定性）与隐状态几何熵（全局不确定性），且仅需单次前向传播。

Result: 在三个模型系列和六个基准测试中，GLU 优于现有的无监督基线方法，能够捕捉不同类型的失效模式，且具备长度归一化和架构无关性。

Conclusion: 提出的GLU方法通过融合局部语义不确定性和全局几何复杂度，为LLM提供了一种高效、无监督且精准的置信度评估手段，有效解决了模型自信但错误的不确定性量化痛点。

Abstract: Large language models hallucinate confidently, making uncertainty quantification (UQ) essential for reliable deployment. Existing methods rely predominantly on token-level signals, leaving the geometric structure of intermediate hidden states underused. In this paper, we take the geometric complexity of hidden-state matrices as a measure of the global uncertainty of LLMs, while treating token-level uncertainty estimation as a local metric. We show that hidden-state geometric entropy (global uncertainty) and token-level entropy (local uncertainty) are statistically near-orthogonal, capturing distinct failure regimes for reliability prediction. In particular, global geometry recovers the confident-but-wrong failure mode that local signals systematically miss. Building on this, we propose Global-Local Uncertainty (GLU), an unsupervised, single-pass score that fuses the two signals via a multiplicative gate. Across three model families and six benchmarks, GLU matches or outperforms all unsupervised baselines while requiring only a single forward pass and remaining length-normalized and architecture-agnostic.

</details>


### [249] [QSplitFL: Capability Aware Deep Q-Learning for Optimal Split Point Selection in Split Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09869)
*Nazmus Shakib Shadin,Xinyue Zhang,Jingyi Wang,Miao Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QSplitFL 是一种感知硬件能力的 DQN 框架，通过轻量级状态表示和委员会机制，解决了 SFL 环境中分割点选择的动态优化问题，提升了异构设备下的训练效率与模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在联合分割学习（SFL）中，固定的模型分割点导致异构硬件资源下客户端负载不均匀，进而引发通信瓶颈、训练缓慢及性能不稳定。现有方法常依赖高维权重表示，计算开销过大。

Method: 提出 QSplitFL 框架，利用深度 Q 网络（DQN）进行分割点选择。该方法使用基于本地硬件指标（CPU、内存、电量、网络延迟）的轻量级状态表示，采用衰减损失下降奖励函数，并结合基于委员会（Majority Voting）的 DQN 架构以防止奖励欺骗。

Result: 在多个数据集（MNIST, CIFAR等）和多种主流神经网络模型（ResNet50, MobileNetV4等）上进行测试，结果表明 QSplitFL 相较于现有方法，在模型收敛性、准确率及对于异构硬件的适应性方面表现更优。

Conclusion: QSplitFL 框架能够有效根据异构客户端的硬件能力自适应选择最优分割点，在保证隐私的前提下，显著提升了 SFL 的收敛速度、系统稳定性和预测准确率。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) combined with Split Learning (SL) is a privacy preserving paradigm that enables training deep neural networks (DNNs) on resource constrained devices while reducing overall training cost. However, determining the optimal split point, meaning the layer where the model is divided still remains a critical challenge, especially when clients have heterogeneous hardware capabilities. Fixed split points can overload weak devices and increase the communication and server load, which slows convergence and reduces stability. This paper introduces QSplitFL, a novel capability-aware Deep Q-Network (DQN) framework for optimal split point selection in Split learning based Federated Learning (SFL) environments. Unlike existing approaches that rely on high-dimensional model weight representations, QSplitFL employs a lightweight state representation derived directly from client hardware metrics, including CPU utilization, memory, battery level, and network latency. The proposed framework incorporates a decayed loss-drop reward function that prioritizes early convergence, and a committee-based DQN architecture with majority voting to mitigate reward hacking. Extensive experiments on MNIST, Fashion-MNIST, CIFAR-10, and CIFAR-100 datasets using CNN, ResNet50, MobileNetV4, and ConvNeXt architectures demonstrate that our approach achieves better convergence and higher accuracy compared to existing methods, while effectively adapting to heterogeneous device resources. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/AIPO-Lab/QSplitFL.

</details>


### [250] [Representation Curriculum: Stagewise Training for Robust Ranking and Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09891)
*Ehsan Ebrahimzadeh,Sina Baharlouei,Abraham Bagherjeiran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 为解决数字市场排序模型过度依赖历史暴露偏差导致的冷启动及泛化问题，论文提出了“表征课程学习”（Representation Curriculum, RC），通过分阶段训练强制模型优先利用内容本身的信息，实现了性能与稳健性的双赢。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有排序系统过度依赖历史暴露产生的偏倚信号（如点击率、流行度）。这种预测性的“捷径”虽然在静态环境中有效，但导致了对现有头部品类的强化，损害了模型对新产品（冷启动）的识别能力及在分布偏移情况下的稳健性。

Method: 提出 Representation Curriculum (RC) 训练框架。该方法通过时间分阶段训练：首先聚焦于基于内容的“优点信号”（merit signals），随训练进程逐步引入基于暴露的“信念信号”（belief signals），并同时利用锚定机制（anchoring）防止内容特征向偏差方向漂移。

Result: 在理论分析上，证明了 RC 在特定设置下能显著降低冷启动分布的种群风险；实验验证表明，RC 在多个公开基准测试及大型电商生产系统中，均能有效降低对历史偏倚信号的依赖，提升冷启动任务性能，且仅产生受控的头部性能折损。

Conclusion: Representation Curriculum (RC) 是一种有效的训练策略，能通过显式的时间维度干预，强制模型优先学习内容本身的信息（merit signals），从而缓解对暴露偏差（exposure-dependent belief signals）的依赖，增强模型在冷启动场景的泛化能力和稳健性。

Abstract: Ranking in digital marketplaces is a dynamic exposure-allocation mechanism: displayed items shape discovery trajectories and success events logged by the platform to update future allocation policies. Modern ranking systems rely heavily on exposure-confounded signals (e.g. popularity estimates, CTR/CVR aggregates, and ID-based representation), because they are highly predictive under stationary demand. Yet this predictive power can become a learning shortcut: early access to exposure-dependent belief signals steers optimization toward over-reliance on them and away from exposure-independent merit signals (e.g., content-based competitiveness and semantic affinity). Consequently, the learned policy tends to entrench incumbents and degrade cold-start generalization and robustness under distribution shift. We propose Representation Curriculum (RC), a training-time intervention that temporally stages feature utilization. RC foregrounds content-based merit signals initially, then introduces exposure-dependent belief signals while anchoring the content pathway near the learned merit representation, curbing shortcut reliance on historical signals and mitigating gradient starvation on content signals. We formalize RC independently of task and hypothesis class and provide ranking-specific instantiations. In a Gaussian linear ridge setting, we derive closed-form solutions and sufficient conditions under which RC strictly reduces population risk on a cold-start target distribution, with a quantified Pareto tradeoff against source performance. Experiments on public learning-to-rank and recommendation benchmarks, and randomized online experiments in a large-scale e-commerce search system, show that RC measurably shifts reliance from historical belief signals toward content-based merit signals and yields consistent gains on cold populations with a controlled trade-off in head performance.

</details>


### [251] [LLM-Guided Neural Architecture Search for Robust Co-Design of Physical Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10294)
*Tyler King,Timothee Leleu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: UH-NAS是一个硬件无关的NAS框架，通过LLM引导进化搜索，实现了对不同计算平台（特别是非常规硬件）架构与性能约束的自动协同优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的神经架构搜索（NAS）方法通常针对特定硬件系列定制，难以应对非常规硬件（如光学计算平台）的特殊限制（如能源成本、物理非理想性和精度要求），且缺乏跨平台对比的通用性。

Method: 提出一种硬件无关的、基于大语言模型（LLM）引导的NAS框架。该框架将硬件作为可替换的后端模块（包含能量模型、物理约束和非理想效应模拟器），并使用LLM作为进化算子来自动搜索和优化神经网络架构。

Result: 在光学MZI硬件平台上的测试表明，UH-NAS发现的架构比传统基线更具多样性和鲁棒性，且性能优于现有的LLM-to-NAS方法。

Conclusion: UH-NAS有效证明了硬件无关的进化NAS框架在协同优化精度与异构硬件（如光学MZI）约束方面的有效性，强调了针对新兴计算平台进行架构-硬件协同设计的必要性。

Abstract: Deploying neural networks on unconventional hardware demands architectures that co-optimize task accuracy and platform-specific constraints such as energy cost, physical non-idealities, and numerical precision. Existing neural architecture search (NAS) methods are typically tailored to a single hardware family, limiting cross-platform comparison and generalization. We introduce Unconventional Hardware Neural Architecture Search (UH-NAS), a hardware-agnostic, LLM-guided NAS framework that integrates language models as evolutionary operators to co-optimize accuracy and inference energy. By exposing hardware as a swappable backend with per-platform energy models, physical constraints, and non-ideality simulators, UH-NAS enables fair system-level comparisons across various backends without modifying the search algorithm. Tested on optical MZI hardware, UH-NAS discovers more diverse, robust architectures than conventional baselines while outperforming existing LLM-to-NAS approaches. Additional ablations on architecture robustness under non-idealities and the role of system prompts highlight the importance of architecture-hardware co-design for emerging computing platforms.

</details>


### [252] [TENP: Trapezoidal Expert Neuron Pruning For Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09885)
*Jiangyang He,Shaolin Zhu,Deyi Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 为了解决 MoE 模型参数量巨大导致的部署难题，本文提出了 TENP 框架，通过梯形化结构剪枝技术在保障模型性能（甚至提升代码任务能力）的前提下，大幅减少了静态参数占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE 模型在大规模参数下的部署成本极其高昂，而现有的剪枝方法存在破坏路由拓扑结构影响性能，或因非结构化剪枝导致硬件运行效率低下的问题。

Method: 提出了一种名为 TENP 的梯形专家神经元剪枝框架。包括：1. 专家重要性评估策略（结合输出幅度和输入向量方向改变能力）；2. 专家神经元剪枝 (ENP) 技术（通过测量神经元对输出的投影贡献来保留关键路径）；3. 梯形修剪策略（从浅层到深层保留不同比例参数）。

Result: 在 Qwen 和 DeepSeek 模型上的实验表明，在 40% 的路由专家稀疏性和 63.76% 的平均激活参数稀疏下，DeepSeek 模型仅损失 1% 的精度，并在代码生成任务中性能提升了 10%。

Conclusion: TENP 框架通过梯形结构化剪枝，在有效压缩 Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) 模型参数的同时，显著降低了部署开销，并最大程度保留了模型性能，甚至在代码生成任务上展现出超越原模型的结果。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts large language models (LLMs) scale efficiently through sparse activation, yet their deployment is fundamentally constrained by the large static parameter footprint of experts. Existing compression approaches either remove entire experts, disrupting routing topology and harming performance, or rely on unstructured weight pruning with limited practical efficiency. To address the limitations, we propose TENP, a structured Trapezoidal ExpertNeuron Pruning framework. Using a few samples, we identify and retain important experts, while applying expert neuron pruning (ENP) to less important experts, reserving model parameters in a trapezoidal pattern from shallow to deep layers. When evaluating expert importance, we jointly consider both the magnitude of the expert output and its ability to change the direction of the input vector. For ENP, we measure each neuron's projected contribution to the expert output to identify and retain important neurons. We conduct extensive experiments on the Qwen and DeepSeek models. Under a routing expert sparsity of 40% and an average of 63.76% activated expert parameters, the DeepSeek model suffers only a 1-point drop in accuracy compared to the full-parameter model. Moreover, it outperforms the full-parameter model by 10% on code generation tasks.

</details>


### [253] [PatchSTG: Scalable Spatiotemporal Graph Transformers for Traffic Forecasting on Irregular Sensor Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09872)
*Jichao Li,Xuanming Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PatchSTG 提出了一种基于地理分区和双层注意力机制的时空 Transformer，有效解决了不规则传感器网络中交通预测的扩展性与计算成本难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图模型和基于注意力模型在处理现实世界中不规则的传感器分布时，存在建模效率低、可扩展性差的问题，且难以捕捉大规模时空依赖。

Method: 提出 PatchSTG（基于补丁的时空图 Transformer）：1. 通过地理信息将传感器划分为局部平衡的补丁（Patch）；2. 设计双注意力编码器（交替进行补丁内注意力和补丁间注意力），将计算复杂度从二次方降低至准线性。

Result: PatchSTG 在罗德岛及其他大规模交通数据集上实现了稳定且具有竞争力的预测性能，同时显著提高了计算效率。

Conclusion: 基于分区的时空建模是处理不规则传感器网络下交通预测的一种可扩展且有效的框架。

Abstract: Traffic forecasting is a fundamental component of intelligent transportation systems, yet remains challenging in real-world settings due to irregular sensor distributions and the high computational cost of modeling large-scale spatiotemporal dependencies. In practical traffic networks, sensors are unevenly distributed across regions, leading to non-uniform spatial structures that limit the effectiveness and scalability of existing graph-based and attention-based models. To address these challenges, we propose PatchSTG, a patch-based spatiotemporal graph Transformer designed for efficient forecasting on irregular sensor networks. The key idea is to introduce a hierarchical spatial representation that partitions sensors into balanced, locality-preserving patches based on geographic information. On top of this structure, a dual attention encoder alternates between intra-patch attention for capturing local interactions and inter-patch attention for modeling global dependencies, reducing computational complexity from quadratic to near-linear scaling. We evaluate PatchSTG on real-world traffic data from Rhode Island and additional large-scale datasets. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed model achieves stable and competitive forecasting performance across multiple horizons, while significantly improving computational efficiency. Ablation studies further validate the effectiveness of spatial partitioning and dual attention in capturing both local and long-range traffic dynamics. These results suggest that patch-based spatiotemporal modeling provides a scalable and effective framework for traffic forecasting under irregular spatial settings.

</details>


### [254] [Nonlinear Estimator: Dual Bayesian Affine Estimators for Parameter Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10111)
*Sasan Vakili,Daniël Woonings,Pradyumna Paruchuri,Peyman Mohajerin Esfahani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为Wiener型状态空间模型设计了一种基于固定点迭代的非线性参数估计框架，通过引入动态基统计量（DBS），在保持计算效率的同时，显著提升了参数估计的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Wiener型状态空间模型参数估计方法在复杂性和性能平衡上存在不足，研究旨在开发一种高效、基于固定点架构的非线性参数估计器，以改善参数估计的精度。

Method: 提出了一种基于固定点架构的非线性参数估计方法，通过耦合两个仿射最小均方误差（MMSE）估计器（一个针对未知参数，一个针对潜在变量），并引入动态基统计量（DBS）来总结非线性基函数评估，形成“双基-参数估计器”和“双状态-参数估计器”两种框架。

Result: 蒙特卡洛实验表明，所提出的双状态-参数估计器在均方误差（MSE）指标上显著优于双基-参数估计器、纯仿射参数估计器以及经典的粒子Gibbs和EM算法。

Conclusion: 所提出的双状态-参数估计器（dual state-parameter estimator）在Wiener型状态空间模型的参数估计中表现最优，优于基准方法及现有的粒子Gibbs和EM算法，证实了该固定点架构在处理非线性系统参数估计中的有效性。

Abstract: This paper presents a nonlinear parameter estimator for Wiener-type state-space models obtained as a fixed-point architecture that couples two affine minimum mean-squared error (MMSE) estimators: one for the unknown parameters and one for latent variables. The architecture retains the functional structure of the optimal affine MMSE parameter estimator while incorporating Dynamic Basis Statistics (DBS) estimates that summarize nonlinear basis-function evaluations. Two DBS construction strategies are developed, leading to two nonlinear estimator frameworks.
